The existing per-cell context menu (previously only "Clear cell") gains a **Show edit history** item (clock icon, above a divider) on every cell. Selecting it opens a modal listing that cell's `AuditLog` rows newest-first — editor name, timestamp, and `old → new` value transition — reusing the existing `field-history` endpoint (`GET /export/shipments/{id}/field-history/?field=`), the `useFieldHistory` lazy hook, and the history-row layout (the popover body in `CellLastEditMarker` was extracted to an exported `FieldHistoryContent` component). The modal is mounted only while open and the query fires only on open, so it adds no cost to the ~880 cells rendered at peak. 403 (no edit permission on sensitive fields) degrades to "No history available."; cells with no edits show "No edits recorded yet." New i18n key `sheet.show_history` × tk/ru/en (history modal title/empty/forbidden strings already existed). Backend unchanged. Type-check clean. (`docs/obsidian/screens/shipment-sheet.md` updated.)
- **Work-time logging — Phase 3 of WS feature** (feat(core) + feat(frontend), [ADR-020](docs/ADR.md#adr-020-realtime-stack--django-channels--redis--uvicorn-workers-one-global-sheet-presence-room), [worklog](docs/obsidian/processes/worklog.md)). New `core.work_sessions` table (`core_work_sessions` + secondary indexes on `(user, started_at DESC)` and `(ended_at, last_heartbeat_at)`) plus the `core.work_session_daily` SQL view that sums per user per local date — `WorkSessionDaily` ORM model is `managed=False` and binds to it (migration `core.0017_work_sessions` creates the view via `RunSQL`). `apps/core/services/worklog.py` owns `open_session` / `record_heartbeat` / `patch_session_identity` / `close_session` / `reap_stale`; per-tick add is capped at `min(now - last_heartbeat_at, 2 × WS_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS)` so a long laptop-sleep gap doesn't pad the total. `AppConsumer.connect` auto-opens a session row (so the connect-to-first-frame window counts); the client emits `worklog.heartbeat / start` with a sessionStorage UUID to back-patch `tab_session_id` + `client_info`, then ticks every 30 s. `disconnect` closes the row. Crashed clients are caught by `python manage.py reap_work_sessions` (intended to run from host cron every 60 s) which closes any row with `last_heartbeat_at` older than 120 s, setting `ended_at = last_heartbeat_at` so silent dead time isn't credited. Three REST endpoints, all open to any authenticated user (radical-transparency rule locked in scoping): `GET /api/v1/core/worklog/me/?from=&to=`, `GET /api/v1/core/worklog/?user=&from=&to=`, `GET /api/v1/core/worklog/team/?date=` (the team endpoint includes zero rows for users with no activity so the page shows the full roster). Frontend: `useWorklogHeartbeat({ enabled })` mounted in `AppLayout` (sends `start` on every `open` status transition + ticks while open), `useMyWorklog` / `useTeamWorklog` / `useUserWorklog` TanStack hooks (5-min stale), `WorklogChip` ("Today: 4h 23m") slotted into the header next to `ConnectionStatus`, `/worklog` page with a personal last-7-days card + team-by-day table (sortable, "Active time" defaultSort descending). Sidebar group "Team" → "Iş wagty / Рабочее время / Work hours" entry visible to every authenticated user. 8 backend tests (`apps.core.tests.test_worklog`): heartbeat cap (25 s gap), heartbeat 6 h sleep cap, idempotent close, reaper with mixed live + stale rows, WS connect-opens / disconnect-closes, `/me/` sums today + range, `/list` returns every user, `/team/` includes zero rows. Frontend tsc clean. DDL v5.1 updated with the new table + view; ADR-020 already captured the Phase 3 decisions during the original realtime-stack ADR. New env vars (documented in ADR): `WS_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (default 30), `WS_IDLE_THRESHOLD_SECONDS` (default 120).
- **WebSocket plumbing + Sheet presence avatars** (feat(core) + feat(frontend), [ADR-020](docs/ADR.md#adr-020-realtime-stack--django-channels--redis--uvicorn-workers-one-global-sheet-presence-room), [realtime-presence](docs/obsidian/processes/realtime-presence.md)). Two-phase WebSocket rollout. **Phase 1 (plumbing)**: Django Channels 4 + `channels-redis` (backed by the existing redis service), single multiplexed app socket at `/ws/app/` carrying `{channel, type, payload}` envelopes, `CookieJWTAuthMiddleware` ported from `apps.core.authentication` for the WS handshake (anonymous → close 4401), `AppConsumer` stub with `system.ping`/`system.pong` round trip, `gunicorn -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker config.asgi:application` so one uvicorn process pool serves both `/api/` HTTP and `/ws/` WebSocket on port 8000, `/ws/` block in `frontend/nginx.conf` with `Upgrade`/`Connection` headers + 3600 s read timeout, `daphne` added as test-only dep (channels.testing imports it). **Phase 2 (presence)**: `presence.sheet` channel with one global room (group `presence.sheet`, Redis hash `presence:sheet` keyed by `channel_name → {user_id, name, role, color, joined_at}` so all uvicorn workers see the same roster; in-process dict mirror under `RUNNING_TESTS`), `apps/core/services/presence.py` with `join_sheet`/`leave_sheet`/`roster` + deterministic per-user colour palette. Frontend singleton WS client at `services/realtime.ts` (exponential backoff 1 s → 30 s, `navigator.onLine`-gated, close 4401 is terminal), `realtimeStore` (Zustand: status + sheetRoster), `useRealtime({ enabled })` mounted in `AppLayout` to open the socket on auth resolve, `usePresenceSheet()` joins on Sheet mount + re-joins on every reconnect, `PresenceAvatars` (Ant `Avatar.Group`, max 5 inline + "+N" overflow, "(you)" marker, role-aware tooltips) slotted into `SheetToolbar`'s right cluster. `ConnectionStatus` dot extended: green = online + WS open, yellow = online + WS reconnecting, red = browser offline; tooltip cycles through the matching status. 5 backend tests (`apps.core.tests.test_app_consumer`) cover anonymous reject, authenticated handshake, ping/pong, two-client roster broadcast, and disconnect-shrinks-roster. New i18n keys: `connection.ws_open` / `ws_connecting` / `ws_closed`, `presence.viewing_now` / `you` / `tooltip_role` × tk/ru/en. Frontend type-check clean.
- **All-invoices list page at `/invoices` (P4 Slice C frontend)** (feat(p4)). Standalone `InvoiceList` page (`pages/invoices/InvoiceList.tsx`) with full ProTable: 14 columns including clickable contract number, truncated passport_sdelka with tooltip, `✓/✗` scan indicator, status tag, per-row edit/delete actions. Toolbar: text search (client-side, normalised across contract_number + export_firm_name + import_firm_name + passport_sdelka + shipment_code + invoice_number), `ExportFirmSelect` and `ImportFirmSelect` dropdowns, `DatePicker.RangePicker` for invoice_date, status dropdown, "Add invoice" button — all five URL-synced (`?q=`, `?export_firm=`, `?import_firm=`, `?date_from=`, `?date_to=`, `?status=`). Default sort: newest `invoice_date` first (client-side, `defaultSortOrder: 'descend'`). Row click navigates to the parent contract. Create modal uses `InvoiceCreate` in new standalone mode: `ContractSelect` self-fetching dropdown as first field, `last_invoice_number` auto-fetched via `useContract` and pre-filled as `last_invoice_number + 1` (auto-fill moved to `useEffect` — StrictMode safe). Sidebar "Şertnamalar" group — second item after contracts list. New `ContractSelect` self-fetching component (`components/ContractSelect.tsx`). New i18n keys: `nav.invoices.list`, `invoices_list.*`, `invoices.create.field.contract`, `contracts.select.placeholder` × tk/ru/en. Backend changes: `last_invoice_number` added to `ContractListSerializer.Meta.fields`; `InvoiceViewSet` ordering changed to `[-invoice_date, contract_id, invoice_number]`; `pagination_class = None` added (bare array response — all invoices loaded client-side; `useInvoices` hook normalises the response shape). Obsidian doc: `screens/invoice-list.md`. Type-check clean.
- **Contracts detail page with Faktura tab + invoice create/edit/delete (Slice B frontend of P4)** (feat(p4)). `/contracts/:id` now renders a full `Descriptions` header (contract terms + denormalized totals grouped by Planlanan / Eksport edilen / Galan / Tölegler) and four `Tabs`: Faktura (fully built), Tölegler / Passport sdelkalary / Kommentarlar (placeholder stubs). `InvoicesTab` ProTable: 12 columns, toolbar "Faktura goş" button, admin-only delete with `Popconfirm`. `InvoiceCreate` modal handles both CREATE and EDIT (single component, `editingInvoice` prop). Auto-compute `total_usd = qty × price` with `userManuallyEditedTotal` ref so manual override wins. DRF field-level errors mapped to `Form.Item`. Both `['invoices']` and `['contracts']` invalidated on every mutation so the header totals refresh. `nextInvoiceNumber` derived from `Math.max` of loaded invoices (backend `last_invoice_number` not serialized — documented). New types, hooks, i18n `invoices.*` + `contracts.detail.*` × tk/ru/en. Obsidian doc: `screens/contract-detail.md`. Type-check clean.
- **Contracts Invoice model + rollup service + invoice REST API (Slice B backend of P4)** (feat(p4)). `Invoice` model (`contracts_invoice` table), `rollup_contract_totals()` service, three serializers (`InvoiceListSerializer`, `InvoiceDetailSerializer`, `InvoiceCreateSerializer`), `InvoiceViewSet`, and 17 integration tests (all passing). Migration `contracts.0002_alter_contract_options_invoice` applied. Obsidian doc: `reference/contracts-invoice-model.md`.
- **Contracts list page + create modal (Slice A frontend of P4)** (feat(p4)). `/contracts` ProTable with Ant Design grouped columns (Planlanan/Eksport edilen/Galan/Tölegler), "Show ended" URL-synced toggle, client-side search, status filter, `SeasonSelect` component. `/contracts/:id` placeholder Descriptions detail. `ContractCreate` modal with full form + sonner toast + DRF field-level error handling. Types, hooks, i18n × 3, sidebar group "Şertnamalar". Default list ordering changed to oldest-first (`created_at ASC`) to match the Excel top-down typing order. Two post-build fixes: (1) `contract_type` is now omitted from the create payload when blank so the server's `default='EXPORT'` fires instead of receiving `null` (the model field is non-nullable); (2) the create-button label dropped its hardcoded `"+ "` prefix in all three locales because the Antd `<PlusOutlined />` icon already renders one — was double-plus. Type-check clean. (`screens/contract-list.md` added.)
- **Contracts app — Contract model + list/create/detail API (Slice A of P4)** (feat(p4)). New `apps.contracts` app with `Contract` model (`contracts_contract` table), three serializers (`ContractListSerializer`, `ContractDetailSerializer`, `ContractCreateSerializer`), `ContractViewSet`, and 13 integration tests (all passing). Status enum: active/completed/closed/cancelled. Default list endpoint returns only `active` contracts; `?include_ended=true` adds completed+closed; `cancelled` is never returned. Computed properties: `trucks_remaining`, `quantity_remaining_kg`, `amount_remaining_usd`, `percent_consumed`, `ostatok_usd`. `remaining_usd` auto-computed in `save()` as placeholder until the Slice B rollup service takes ownership. Migration `contracts.0001_initial` applied. Obsidian doc: `reference/contracts-contract-model.md`.


- **Feedback admin inbox: Delete ticket** (feat(frontend)). `TicketDetailPanel` gains a danger-styled **Delete** button (DeleteOutlined + `Popconfirm` two-step confirm) in the actions row, visible only when `useAuth().user.role === 'admin'`. Calls the existing `DELETE /api/v1/feedback/tickets/{id}/` endpoint (already gated server-side by `IsFeedbackAdmin`), which cascades to replies + attachments via the model FKs. On success the panel emits a new `onDeleted` callback that `AdminInboxPage` uses to clear `selectedId`, dropping the right pane back to the empty state and invalidating the `['feedback']` query family so the list refetches. New `useDeleteTicket(ticketId)` hook in `useFeedback.ts`. i18n: `feedback.action.delete`, `delete_confirm_title`, `delete_confirm_body`, `deleted_toast` × tk/ru/en (reuses existing `common.cancel`). Type-check clean. (`feedback-module.md` updated.)
- **Sheet right-click context menu — Clear cell (foundation for more)** (feat(frontend)). `SheetCell` now wraps **every** non-hidden cell with an Ant `Dropdown` (`trigger={['contextMenu']}`), replacing the browser's native right-click menu with a Sheet-owned one so future actions (Copy value, View history, …) have a home. The only item today is a danger-styled **Clear cell**, which restores a safe path to null a `date`/`datetime` (the picker's X is intentionally disabled per the earlier accidental-wipe regression — see `SheetCellEditor` `allowClear={false}`) without sending users to the Django admin. Dispatches per field type:
  - `custom_*` rows → `PATCH /shipments/{id}/custom-fields/` `{value: ''}`; cache row's `custom_fields[key]` is set to `''` in `onMutate` for instant repaint, snapshot rolled back on error.
  - Multiselect junctions (`firm_splits` / `block_sources`) → `POST .../firm-splits/` or `block-sources/` with the items array empty (server replaces all rows, so empty = delete-all); cache row's `firm_splits` / `block_sources` is set to `[]` in `onMutate` for instant repaint, snapshot rolled back on error. **No post-success sheet invalidate** — those endpoints only return `{status, count}` so the optimistic update IS the truth; refetching here would re-introduce a ~1–2 s wait on a ~1000-row season (the original "needs refresh" symptom).
  - FK fields (`country`, `customer`, `city`, `import_firm`, `variety`, `border_point`, `vehicle_responsible`) → `PATCH /shipments/{id}/` `{field: null}` via `useShipmentPatch`, plus a companion-field wipe right before the patch fires (nulls cached `country_name`/`country_code`/`country_color`/etc.) so the cell flips empty on the next render instead of waiting for the PATCH response's `reconcileFromServer` ~200–500 ms later. The reconcile step still runs and overwrites with the authoritative response.
  - Other scalar fields → plain `PATCH /shipments/{id}/` `{field: null}` via `useShipmentPatch`, optimistic+reconcile path unchanged.

  The Clear item is **disabled (greyed out)** rather than the menu being suppressed when clearing doesn't apply (already-empty, non-editable, `shipment_code`, `has_doc_advance`/`has_sales_report` computed, bool dropdowns `peregruz`/`gornushi`, reorder mode), so future items can be added without re-arranging the wrapper. Hidden gapy `—` placeholder cells are the only ones that skip the Dropdown entirely. New `sheet.clear_cell` i18n key × 3 locales. Type-check clean. (`shipment-sheet.md` updated.)

### Changed
- **Dropped `weight_gross` from the `fill_loading_data` task triggers** (data(p3)). `weight_gross` was a target field on the `yuklenme` step's `fill_loading_data` TaskRule, but it has **no row in `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS`** — operators have no UI path to enter it from the Sheet, so the task could never auto-resolve through the normal workflow. Updated the rule's `target_fields` to `'shipment_code,block_sources,variety,weight_net'`, ran `seed_task_rules` (auto-reconcile rewrote the task row on the 1 affected shipment), then fired `auto_advance_if_ready` on shipment 0106001/26 — which advanced `yuklenme → yola_chykdy` (stopped there because `border_crossed_at` is still NULL, as expected for a truck mid-route). `weight_net` remains the operational ground truth for the loading step. If `weight_gross` becomes operationally needed later, add a Sheet row + restore it to `target_fields` — both halves needed for the auto-resolve path to work end-to-end.
- **Removed `quality_inspection` task from the `yuklenme` step** (data(p3)). The 4-quality-flag check (`azyk_maglumatnama` + `suriji_gozukdiriji` + `hil_sertifikaty` + `kalibrowka_analiz`) is no longer a gate for advancing `yuklenme → yola_chykdy`. Operationally the greenhouse team marks quality docs elsewhere; gating the lifecycle on those four flags was blocking real shipments whose paperwork is tracked outside the Sheet. Dropped the rule entry from `TASK_RULES` in `seed_task_rules.py`, set `is_active=False` on the existing DB rule (id 8 — preserved for audit; soft-disable rather than delete so historical Task rows still resolve their `rule_id`), and cancelled the 1 currently-open `quality_inspection` Task so cascades through `yuklenme` can complete. Future shipments entering `yuklenme` won't generate this task (`generate_tasks_for_status` filters `is_active=True`). i18n keys for `tasks.quality_inspection` left in place so historical (cancelled) tasks still display a label. Obsidian: `reference/task-rules.md` row removed.

### Fixed
- **Sheet R26 — Transit Days & Temp combined cell is now editable** (fix(p3) + fix(frontend) + fix(core)). The combined `transit_days_temp` row at R26 was `input_type='readonly'` and rendered `${days}d ${temp}°C` as a display-only string — operators couldn't enter values. Made the row editable as a virtual combined cell: one text input edits BOTH `transit_days` and `transport_temp_c` in a single PATCH. **Backend**: `sheet_rows.py` R26 flipped to `input_type='text'` with `default_who_key='sheet.who.transport'` (was `sheet.who.quality` — that who-slug had no role mapping so the existing `SheetRowSetting` had zero triggers and zero localized labels). `can_edit_sheet_field` gained a `_VIRTUAL_FIELD_DELEGATES = {'transit_days_temp': 'transit_days'}` map at the top so the perm gate for the virtual key delegates to the real `transit_days` field's perm chain (which routes to `RoleFieldPermission` for `transport`). `audit_orphan_sheet_rows.py` gained a `KNOWN_VIRTUAL` set listing `transit_days_temp` so the auditor doesn't flag the virtual key as missing from `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS` (the patch targets the two real fields, not the virtual key). **Frontend**: `SheetCellEditor` special-cases `field_key === 'transit_days_temp'` BEFORE the `input_type` switch — renders an Ant `Input` with the current values pre-filled as `"${days} ${temp}"`, parses two numbers from whatever the user types via `/-?\d+(\.\d+)?/g` (so `"5 4"`, `"5d 4°C"`, `"5/4"` all work), then dispatches `useShipmentPatchMulti` with `{transit_days, transport_temp_c}` as a single PATCH body. Empty input clears both fields. **Deploy step**: run `python manage.py backfill_sheet_row_defaults` after deploy — the existing `transit_days_temp` `SheetRowSetting` row has empty labels/who (legacy artifact of the unmapped `who.quality` slug) and no role triggers; backfill seeds all three locales' labels/who from i18n + the `transport` role trigger (add-only, idempotent).
- **Auto-advance now cascades through every pre-satisfied step (was strictly one step per save)** (fix(p3)). `auto_advance_if_ready` used to fire exactly one `transition_to` per outer `Shipment.save()` — the model's thread-local re-entry guard prevented the inner save inside `transition_to` from cascading. That was fine when users fill one trigger field at a time, but it left shipments stuck whenever multiple downstream triggers were already filled before the status caught up: a TaskRule edit + reconcile, a backfill / import, or — most acutely — a draft whose `customs_exit_at`, `loading_started_at`, `departed_at` etc. were all entered days before the `start_documents_prep` rule fix landed. After the rule fix, those shipments stepped exactly one notch out of draft into `gumruk_girish` and stopped, even though the next 2–3 steps' triggers were already satisfied. **The fix:** `auto_advance_if_ready` now loops at its own level (NOT via save recursion — the re-entry guard stays in place to prevent infinite recursion through `Shipment.save`). Each iteration checks `is_step_trigger_satisfied`, calls `transition_to(notify=False)` if yes, and repeats. The loop terminates when an unsatisfied step is hit, when the current status has no outgoing edge (terminal / cancelled), or when it hits `MAX_CHAIN=13` (defensive cap; the status graph is acyclic so never expected). Each cascaded transition writes its own `ShipmentStatusLog` (flagged `is_auto=True`) and `AuditLog` row — the audit trail captures every step individually. Only the FINAL step's `_notify_action_required` fires (intermediate roles' data is already filled, so re-notifying them would be noise). `transition_to` gained a `notify: bool = True` keyword for this. **Verified against shipment 0106001/26**: one call cascaded `gumruk_girish → gumruk_chykysh → yuklenme` in one save, stopping at `yuklenme` because `fill_loading_data` requires `weight_net` + `weight_gross` which are still NULL on that shipment. **Test updates**: `tests_auto_advance.py` — the old `ReentryGuardTests.test_single_step_only` (which explicitly asserted strict-single-step) was replaced with `CascadeTests.test_cascades_when_multiple_triggers_satisfied`, asserting that pre-filling `customs_exit_at` + `loading_started_at` + `departed_at` in one save walks the shipment all the way to `yuklenme` and writes two new audit log rows in order. The two `DraftAutoAdvanceTests` cases were also fixed: they used `driver_id = 99` to "satisfy" `assign_driver`, but that task actually targets `driver_name + driver_phone + truck_plate` — pre-existing test bug that didn't surface because the assertion was only ever checking whether the single FIELD_EQUALS rule satisfied. All 6 auto_advance tests now pass; clean across `tests_task_engine` + `tests_task_reconcile` + `tests_task_seed` too (95/96; the one error is a pre-existing test attempting an illegal `draft → yuklenme` direct jump, unrelated).
- **Draft auto-advance unblocked: `start_documents_prep` rule now targets `documents_status='ready'`** (fix(p3)). The `tasks.start_documents_prep` TaskRule on the `draft` step targeted `documents_status == 'in_progress'` via `FIELD_EQUALS`, but operators naturally walk `pending → in_progress → ready`, and once the value moved past `'in_progress'` the literal-string check could never re-fire — leaving drafts stuck even after every other draft task was DONE (e.g. shipment `0106001/26`). Changed `target_value` from `'in_progress'` to `'ready'` in `seed_task_rules.py`, then ran `seed_task_rules` (auto-runs `reconcile_open_tasks_with_rules`) which rewrote `target_value` on 38 open Tasks across 38 draft shipments and auto-resolved 1 whose `documents_status` was already `'ready'`. Triggered `auto_advance_if_ready` once for shipment `0106001/26` to flush the resolved state into an actual `draft → gumruk_girish` transition (audited `is_auto=True`). No bulk sweep needed — no other drafts were in the "fully task-satisfied but stuck" state. Obsidian: `reference/task-rules.md` updated.
- **Sheet datetime cell — OK button now saves the default value** (fix(frontend)). On any `input_type='datetime'` cell (R19/R20/R21/R25/R30/R31/R32/R35/R41/R42 + the new R4 `transport_docs_given_at`), pressing the picker's **OK** button without first scrolling the time wheels silently dropped the value: the `defaultValue` (`dayjs()` = now when the cell is empty) only flowed through `onChange`, which fires on user interaction — pressing OK without changes left no `onChange`, so the subsequent `onOpenChange(false)` closed the editor with nothing to commit. Added `onOk` to the datetime DatePicker that mirrors the `onChange` save path, so one-tap "open → OK" now commits the current displayed time. Date-only cells (R39/R43) and the multi-block harvest popover are unaffected (no OK button / explicit Save button respectively).

### Changed
- **Forgiving firm / customer search in dropdowns** (feat(frontend)). `ExportFirmSelect`, `ImportFirmSelect`, and `CustomerSelect` now use a shared `normalizeSearch()` utility (`frontend/src/utils/normalizeSearch.ts`) that lowercases, strips Latin diacritics via NFD + combining-mark removal (so `Gök` is reachable by typing `Gok`, `ş` by `s`, `ğ` by `g`, …), and treats every non-letter / non-digit character (straight and curly quotes, dashes, dots, parentheses, slashes) as whitespace. Each option also carries a precomputed `searchBlob` that concatenates **all** searchable fields — for `ExportFirmSelect` that's `code + name_tk + name_ru + name_en`; for `ImportFirmSelect` it's `code + name_short + name_company`; for `CustomerSelect` just `name` — so the dropdown matches by code as well as by the displayed name. Option labels now suffix the code with a middle dot (`Gök bulut HJ · YGT`, `"Nur-alem" JÇJ · Nur-Alem`) so users see what to type; for ImportFirm the code is only appended when it differs from the short name. Cyrillic and other scripts are preserved as-is (only Latin diacritics decompose). Type-check clean. Used everywhere these selects are referenced — Slice A's `ContractCreate`, the Sheet drawers, ShipmentList filters, and the Edit Shipment forms.
- **Sheet R4: Malik "Goşmaça bellik" → Şirin "Transport bölümiň resminamalary"** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend), per in-app feedback #9). Row 4 was Malik's free-text additional-notes cell (`Shipment.notes`) — repurposed as a datetime cell owned by Şirin (`document_team`) that logs the moment the transport department handed shipment docs over. New model field `Shipment.transport_docs_given_at` (`DateTimeField`, null=True; migration `export 0026`). `sheet_rows.py` R4 retargeted to the new field with `input_type='datetime'`. `getCellValue.ts` special-cases this field so the empty state renders **"Berilmedi"** (not the generic em-dash); filling the datepicker implies "Berildi at this time". Permission grant: `document_team` editable. Surfaced on `ShipmentSheetSerializer`, `ShipmentListSerializer` (so column-manager opt-in works on the list page too), `ShipmentPatchSerializer`'s `_SHEET_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, and `swap_config` / `swapFieldGroups` under the Şirin group. Legacy `Shipment.notes` column is **preserved** on the model + Detail view + `document_team` perms for historical data; it is no longer surfaced on the Sheet. New i18n keys `sheet.row.transport_docs_given` + `sheet.transport_docs_berilmedi` × tk/ru/en. Mocks updated. Backend `manage.py check` clean; frontend type-check clean against the changed file (the only TS errors belong to an untracked `CellColorsTab.tsx`). **Backend tests NOT run in this environment** (MSSQL test DB setup blocks on stdin in this shell). **Deploy step: run the `export` migration so `transport_docs_given_at` exists, then `seed_permissions` (idempotent).**

### Added
- **Sheet "Çalyş" (Swap) — exchange field values between two shipment columns** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). A new toolbar mode that mirrors the existing Join workflow but, instead of merging draft columns, **exchanges (A↔B) a selectable list of field values** between any two shipments of any status. Workflow: click the **Çalyş** toolbar button (sibling to Join, no role gate — per-field edit permissions are enforced at submit time), click two shipment column headers (they get an orange ring; clicking a third replaces the oldest in FIFO), then click Çalyş in the action bar to open the **SwapFieldsModal** — a grouped checklist where **Soltanmyrat** (warehouse: export_code, harvest_status, warehouse_note, loading_started/ended_at, rejected_weight_kg, variety, harvest_date) and **Transport** (vehicle_condition, vehicle_live_status, vehicle_responsible, truck_plate, driver_name, driver_phone, transport_temp_c, vehicle_condition_note) are expanded by default, with **Gadam** (export_manager_note, country, customer, city, import_firm), **Şirin** (documents_status, document_note, customs_exit_at, customs_clearance_planned_day), **Arap** (border_point, border_crossed_at, dest_entry_at, customs_entry_at, has_peregruz, peregruz_*, arrived_at, sale_started/ended_at, sales_report_date, additional_notes_arap), and **Other** (notes, departed_at, transit_days, weight_gross, packaging_kg, pallet_count, box_count) collapsed below. Each row previews both shipments' current values side-by-side with a ⇄ separator (FK companions like `country_name` for display; dayjs-formatted datetimes; Yes/No for booleans; dim "—" for nulls), with per-group and top-level Select/Deselect all toggles. The three modes (Join, Swap, column-Reorder) are mutually exclusive in the store — entering one clears the others. Backend: new `swap_config.SWAPPABLE_FIELDS` whitelist (44 fields; `shipment_code` excluded as auto-generated unique, `weight_net` excluded as block_sources-derived, junction tables / status / audit fields excluded), `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/{a_id}/swap/` `{other_id, fields[]}` → `{shipments: [<DetailA>, <DetailB>], swapped_fields: [...]}`, locks both rows in pk order via `select_for_update`, re-validates soft-delete under lock, skips fields where A == B (no-op returns 200 with `swapped_fields: []` and no audit log), writes one `ShipmentStatusLog` on each side (status unchanged), notifies each shipment's `created_by` (skipped if same as request user). FK fields swap the `_id` integer (not the related object). Per-field gate via the existing `can_edit_sheet_field(user, field_key)` from `apps.core.permissions`; 403 includes the offending field name so the modal can surface it. Companion `GET /api/v1/export/shipments/swappable-fields/` returns the whitelist (no DB) so the modal can `console.warn` on drift between frontend/backend sets without blocking the UI. New `sheet.swap`, `sheet.swap_bar`, `sheet.swap_modal` i18n namespaces × 3 locales (tk/ru/en). Frontend type-check clean. 13 backend tests in `tests_shipment_swap.py` cover happy path, no-op, whitelist rejection (shipment_code + weight_net), self-swap, missing other, empty fields, NULL handling, FK swap, permission denial, partial no-op (mixed equal+different), and the swappable-fields endpoint. **Backend tests NOT run in this environment** (no MSSQL test DB available locally) — run `python manage.py test apps.export.tests_shipment_swap -v 2` before merging.

### Data
- **Imported weekly harvest plans + truck allocations from `data/weekly_plan.xlsx`** (data(p3) + refactor(p3)). The `import_weekly_plan` command was stale — it still wrote the retired wide `monday_plan_kg…saturday_plan_kg` columns and `actual_weekly_total_kg` on `WeeklyHarvestPlan`, which now only carries the (season, block, week, year) header; per-day values moved to `HarvestDayEntry`. Rewrote it to create `WeeklyHarvestPlan` headers + one `HarvestDayEntry` per day carrying `plan_value` (cols C-H), deriving `entry_date` from the sheet's header-row dates and ISO week/year from each week's Monday so imported plans share the same `get_or_create` key `daily_board` uses (dedupe, not duplicate). The weekly-actual column (J) is no longer imported — actuals are per-day and sourced from shipment rollup, and a weekly total can't be split back into days. Sheet name also changed (`Hepdelik planlama` → `Hepdelik maşyn sany`). **Behavior changed from reset-and-import to fill-empties**: nothing is deleted, an existing plan cell is filled only when `plan_value IS NULL`, and operator-entered forecasts/actuals/daily-board data are never touched (idempotent). Run against the beta DB (active season 2025-2026): **44 new plans, 278 new + 393 filled day-entry plan cells, 1905 left untouched, 41 new + 19 filled truck allocations, 120 new destination splits** — the 97 forecasts / 23 actuals / 38 daily-board rows verified unchanged. The sibling `import_harvest_plans` command is still on the old schema and would fail the same way if re-run.
- **ImportFirm backfill: `name_short` from `code`, `country` from address text** (data(core)). New `backfill_import_firm_short_and_country` management command runs two passes on `core.ImportFirm`: (1) `name_short := code` for every row whose `code` is set (codes are the canonical short labels; prior `name_short` values were a mix of long forms and nulls). (2) `country` FK is inferred from the `address` text for rows where `country IS NULL`, by scanning for multilingual keywords (TM/RU/EN/native) per supported country. Picks the country whose earliest keyword hit has the smallest index — so a firm whose primary address mentions one country and consignee another resolves to whichever appears first. `--reevaluate` flag re-checks rows that already have a country (useful after adding new keywords / new Country rows). Final state after run + the companion country seed below: **130/133 firms classified** (97.7%); the remaining 3 have blank addresses (ids 14, 16, 21). Idempotent + `--dry-run`. Executed against production beta DB.
- **Seeded 4 new Country rows: Azerbaijan, Romania, Ukraine, UAE** (data(core)). Added `AZ` / `RO` / `UA` / `AE` to `core.countries` (sort_order 8-11) so the 5 import firms whose primary legal addresses sit outside the original CIS+AF set could be classified. Re-ran the ImportFirm backfill with `--reevaluate`: ids 39 (RO), 40 (AZ), 73 (AZ), 75 (AZ, flipped from incorrect KZ — its consignee was in KZ but the firm itself is in Baku), 128 (UA), 137 (AE). Final per-country firm counts: KZ 27, RU 35, KG 35, BY 1, UZ 17, TJ 8, AF 1, AZ 3, RO 1, UA 1, AE 1.
- **Cleaned up ExportFirm + ImportFirm tables from `data/export_import_firms.xlsx`** (data(p3)). Three management commands run end-to-end against the production beta DB. **(1) `import_firms_from_excel`** (UPSERT). Reads the two-sheet workbook (`export`: 28 firms with TK/RU/EN names, addresses, bank details, director; `import`: 131 buyer/importer firms with TK short names + RU legal name/address/bank details). Natural keys: `ExportFirm.code` for export side (4 Excel rows with no code skipped: Х.О "Милли Хасыл", Какаджанов Х., ИП Чарыгулыев, ИП Саларов); for ImportFirm, stripped `code` if present (trailing spaces normalised, e.g. `'Bukhorzoda Begenc '`), else exact `name_company` match. Per-cell hygiene: strip whitespace, strip trailing underscores from director signature-line fillers, strip trailing commas from `name_tk`. Per-field truncation against the model `max_length` constraints (none triggered on this dataset). `name_tk` is required so rows with a code but blank Turkmen name fall back to `name_ru → name_en`. `ImportFirm.country` left NULL (no country column in this Excel; the companion `backfill_import_firm_short_and_country` command handles that). Three known duplicate codes in the Excel (`Bukhorzoda Begenc`, `Manufaktura`, `ОсОО Насип Жол`) collapse correctly because the second occurrence finds the first by name and UPDATEs. Idempotent + `--dry-run`. Result: ExportFirm 19→38, ImportFirm 20→133; 5 ExportFirms and 12 ImportFirms updated in place (string fields only — FK references preserved). **(2) `merge_duplicate_firms`** (ExportFirm dedup). The Excel upsert left placeholder OLD rows (`GOKBOLUT`, `AKBULUT`, `MIWELIATY`, `YUMAK`, `YGTYBARLY`, `ISGARHJ`, `TELAMANG`, `TELDOWRA16`, `TELDOWRAN`, `TELHEMID10`, `Tel Hem C`, `TELJUMAMY`, `TELGURBAN` — 13 in total) sitting alongside the canonical NEW rows (`GB`, `AB`, `MA`, …) because the OLD codes don't match the Excel keys. All five FK relations on ExportFirm are `PROTECT`, so the user couldn't delete by hand. The merge walks every relation per pair: `greenhouse.DomesticSale.export_firm` and `export.QuotaUsageRecord.export_firm` (no uniqueness — plain `UPDATE`), `export.ShipmentFirmSplit.export_firm` (unique on `(shipment, export_firm)` — sum `weight_kg` + `amount_usd` on conflict, delete OLD junction), `export.QuotaIssuanceFirmAllocation.export_firm` (unique on `(issuance, export_firm)` — sum `kg_quota`), `export.WeeklyLocalSellPlan.export_firm` (unique on `(export_firm, week_number, year)` — sum the six `monday..saturday_plan_kg` columns). Single `transaction.atomic`. Result: 317 `QuotaUsageRecord` rows reassigned (~9.4M kg of quota usage preserved), 90 `QuotaIssuanceFirmAllocation` rows reassigned, 197 `WeeklyLocalSellPlan` rows reassigned + 6 week-collisions summed, 3 `ShipmentFirmSplit` rows reassigned, 13 OLD ExportFirm rows deleted. ExportFirm count 38→25. **(3) `merge_duplicate_import_firms`** (ImportFirm dedup). Same pattern but ImportFirm only has two reference points (`export.Shipment.import_firm` FK `SET_NULL` — plain UPDATE; `core.Customer.import_firms` M2M — per-customer add-then-remove loop, with "already had NEW" tallied separately as collapsed). 17 pairs total: 12 confident NULL-code → Excel canonical (`Aranşy KZ` → `TОО «Араншы-KZ»`, `NUR ALEM` → `Nur-Alem`, `Eko-Bay Keyji` → `ООО «Эко-Бай Кейджи»`, `LLC "Aries Line"` + `Aries layn` → `ОсОО «Ариес Лайн»`, `LLC «Glavryba»` → `ОсОО «Главрыба»`, `Exportlink` → `OOO «EXPORTLINK»`, `MTLK ISHENIM` → `ОсОО «МТЛК Ишеним»`, `Trust industry` → `TRUST INDUSTRY`, `Krasnyy Apelsin` → `ОсОО «КРАСНЫЙ АПЕЛЬСИН»`, `Freshworld Trade` → `ООО «FRESHWORLD TRADE»`, `Dar Zemli` → `ООО «Дар земли»`); 3 user-confirmed ambiguous pairs (`Tel Tursynbayew` → the single `ИП ТУРСЫНБАЕВ` row in DB, `Dargo-88` → `LLC DARGOH88`, `Trans Asia Trade` → `ТОО "TransAsia Trade" (Bagtyyar)`); 2 cross-Excel duplicates merged into the cleaner-code canonical (`LLC «Tauminoti Aulo»` → id 130 `Tauminoti Aulo`; `OcOO "Town Express Company"` → id 132 `TOWN Express Company`). Result: 1 customer M2M junction reassigned, 17 OLD ImportFirm rows deleted. ImportFirm count 133→116.
- **Re-imported quota data from the richer `data/quota/quota.xlsx` (all three streams)** (data(p3)). The new file is far larger than the old `data/quota.xlsx` Task-7 source, so the three importers were retargeted to its layout and a shared `_quota_import_utils.py` (firm resolver + mixed-date parser, fixing year-typos like `07.05.2025`→2026 and separator typos like `18/.04.2026`) now backs all three. **(1) Issued** (`import_quotas`, sheet `Kwota-2` "CYKAN KWOTA", tomato rows 9-25 / pepper rows 9-10) → 19 `QuotaIssuance` events + 157 firm allocations. **(2) Used** (`import_quota_usage`, "Islenen Kwota", tomato rows 33-108 / pepper rows 32-39) → 560 `QuotaUsageRecord` rows (~19.77M kg, now `status='approved'` so the dashboard's used-total counts them; 17 shipment-linked rows preserved). **(3) Local sales** (`import_local_sales`, sheet 2 `Kwota ucin icerki bazara berlen`, 151 daily columns folded into ISO-week Mon-Sat) → 3,008,816 kg (exact match to the sheet's grand total), 54 created / 219 updated via non-destructive `update_or_create`. Fixed a **firm-mapping bug** in the prior local-sales import that misattributed `Tel G Amangeldiyew`→firm #8 (Tel Guwanc A.) and `Tel GJ`→firm #13 (Tel Jumamyradow G); correct targets are #19 / #18 (verified: `Tel ED`→Tel Dowranow E totals matched exactly). New `cleanup_mislabeled_local_sales` command deleted the 14 stale import-origin `approved` rows on firms #8/#13 (the ~172,600 kg double-count) while preserving 4 empty user-entered drafts. All commands keep a `--commit`-guarded dry-run default.
- **Imported 442 May shipments from `docs/shipments/shipments.xlsx`** (data(p3)). New `import_sheet_shipments` management command reads the operational **transposed** "YGT" sheet (each column = one shipment, rows 1-47 = fields) and imports only May (`MY`) shipment codes into the active 2025-2026 season — 442 shipments + 608 firm splits + 627 block sources + 523 `varieties_dominant` M2M links. Populates the Shipment Sheet (which was empty). Status is **derived** from how far each row's timestamp chain is filled (sales report → `tamamlandy`, … down to `yuklenme`) rather than forcing all to complete, since May is the current month and many trucks are still in flight (91 complete / 222 customs-exit / 98 border / 19 loading / 2 transit). Set directly on the model (not via `transition_to()`) — historical bulk import. Auto-created 2 missing `ExportFirm` rows (`TELGURBAN` "Tel Gurban J", `TELAMANG` "Tel Amangeldiyew G"). Robust parsing of TM datetime strings, harvest-date ranges, `"N gün T"` transit/temp, multi-firm/multi-block/multi-variety operator shorthand; per-row insert fallback so one bad row can't sink a batch.

### Changed
- **Sheet: column-color and soft-delete opened to every authenticated viewer** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). Both Sheet column-header actions were previously gated narrowly: the **color-dot** required `canEditField(user, 'shipment', 'column_color')` (only roles with a wildcard shipment grant — `admin`, `director`, `export_manager`), and the **trash icon** was hardcoded to `user.is_superuser || user.role === 'admin'` on both sides. Per stakeholder ask ("any role who can see the sheet table"), both now require only authentication. Frontend `SheetColumnHeader.tsx` drops the `canEditField`/`role === 'admin'` checks (`canPaint`/`canSoftDelete = !!user`). Backend `ShipmentViewSet` got a `get_permissions()` override that swaps `DynamicResourcePermission` for `IsAuthenticated` on three actions listed in `_OPEN_ACTIONS = {soft_delete, restore, set_column_color}`. The `_require_admin` helper is removed from `soft_delete` and `restore`. **Column tint moved to a dedicated `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/{id}/set-column-color/`** endpoint instead of the shared shipment PATCH — required because PATCH is gated by `DynamicResourcePermission` (PATCH → `shipment.can_edit`), which 403s roles like `accountant` / `weight_master` / `boss` *before* the per-field validator could run, even after a serializer-side bypass. The new action accepts `{color: "#RRGGBB" | null | ""}`, rejects edits to deleted/archived rows, defensively truncates to 7 chars, and writes a diff-based `AuditLog` row only when the value actually changes. New frontend hook `useSetColumnColor` (`useShipments.ts`) reuses `useShipmentPatch`'s `applyOptimistic` / `reconcileFromServer` / `rollback` (now exported) so the cell still paints instantly with rollback-on-error. `ShipmentPatchSerializer.validate()` also bypasses `column_color` in the field-perm check (defensive — covers any code path that still PATCHes the field, e.g. `useShipmentPatchMulti`'s Edit Drawer). The `get_queryset` `?show_deleted=true` filter is also opened to every authenticated user (the UI checkbox on `ShipmentList` stays admin-only, but a non-admin who soft-deleted a Sheet column can recover it via the URL param → restore). Type-check + `manage.py check` clean; no backend tests in this area (none existed). **Verification recommendation (not done in this env):** impersonate a `transport` user, POST `/shipments/{id}/soft-delete/` and `/shipments/{id}/set-column-color/ {"color":"#ff0000"}` — both should return 200 (were 403 before).

- **Sheet row 46 renamed "Ýük kody" → "Export kody"** (feat(frontend)). The `export_code` row label (`sheet.row.shipment_code`) changed to **"Export kody"** (tk) / "Экспортный код" (ru) / "Export Code" (en).

### Added
- **Sheet "Ýük goş" one-click empty supply column** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). Replaced the modal-based **"Üpjünçilik sütüni"** supply button (and its `SupplyDraftModal` forecast-pool block picker, now removed) with a **"Ýük goş"** (Add cargo) toolbar button that **one-click** creates an **empty** draft shipment column: new hook `useCreateEmptyColumn` POSTs `{is_draft: true}` to the existing `/export/shipments/` create endpoint (no backend change — `shipment_code` auto-generates, `date` defaults to today, drafts no longer require blocks). Soltanmyrat types the column's values directly into the Sheet cells afterward, then merges it with Gadam's destination column via the existing Join flow; the column renders green because `created_by_role ∈ {loading_dept_head, warehouse_chief}`. Visible to the same roles as the old supply button (`loading_dept_head`, `warehouse_chief`, `export_manager`, `director`, superuser). New `sheet.add_cargo.*` i18n keys × 3 locales (the now-unused `sheet.new_supply.*` keys are left in place, harmless). Type-check clean. (`draft-shipments.md`, `shipment-sheet.md` updated.)
- **Daily Harvest Board — "Ýük plan we galyndy" page** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). New sidebar item in the Export group, directly after Board, opening a per-block daily table that mirrors the operational Google Sheet: **Bloklar / Düýnki galyndy (yesterday's rest) / Bu günki meýilleşdirilýän ýygym (today's plan) / Jemi (sum) / Bellik (note) / Girizilen senesi / Girizildi**. Both the today-plan and yesterday-rest values, plus the note, are entered inline directly in this table for the selected date (date picker + prev/next/today). `HarvestDayEntry` gains four additive fields — `yesterday_rest_value`, `daily_note` (Cyrillic collation), `daily_entered_at`, `daily_entered_by` — via migration `greenhouse 0006` (plus `chk_hde_rest_gte0`); "today's plan" reuses the existing `forecast_value`, so the board and the Weekly Plan grid stay in sync. New gate-free service `upsert_daily_board()` (creates the parent `WeeklyHarvestPlan`/`HarvestDayEntry` on demand, writes an AuditLog `daily_board_set` entry) — unlike the weekly forecast path it has **no role/window restriction**: any authenticated user with page access may edit. New endpoint `GET/POST /api/v1/greenhouse/daily-plan/` (`DailyHarvestBoardViewSet`): GET returns one row per active block for `?date=` (default today, with season + totals context), POST upserts one block/date cell (`{block, date, today_plan?, yesterday_rest?, note?}`). New page code `export.harvest_board` in the permission registry, seeded visible for all operational roles + greenhouse_manager (hidden for seller/boss); core migration `0012` grants it on existing deployments. Frontend: `DailyHarvestBoard` page, `useDailyBoard`/`useUpsertDailyBoard` hooks, `DailyBoardNumberCell`/`DailyBoardTextCell` inline-edit components (commit on blur/Enter only when changed), `IDailyBoardRow`/`IDailyBoardResponse` types, route `/export/harvest-board`, and a `harvest_board.*` i18n namespace × 3 locales. Type-check clean; backend `makemigrations --check` + `manage.py check` clean (full migrate/tests require MSSQL, unavailable in this env). **Deploy step: run the `greenhouse` + `core` migrations; new page auto-grants to roles via core 0012 (or `seed_permissions`).**
- **Weekly Plan: Sunday-EOD edit cutoff + admin late-edit extension** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). `greenhouse_manager` plan-value edits are now server-blocked after Sunday 23:59:59 Asia/Ashgabat of the week **before** the plan week (i.e. for week-W edits you have through Sun of week W-1). Admin always allowed. New `WeeklyHarvestPlan` fields `late_edit_granted_until` / `_by` / `_at` / `_reason` (migration `greenhouse 0005`, all nullable, additive — no data backfill needed). New service helper `_plan_edit_window_closed(weekly_plan, now=None)` so the rule is testable in isolation; passive expiry (`granted_until > now` comparison, no cron). `set_actual_value()` is intentionally unaffected — warehouse_chief / loading_dept_head can still enter actuals on Monday for the closed week. Four **admin-only** endpoints: per-row `POST /api/v1/greenhouse/harvest-plans/{id}/grant-late-edit/` + `…/revoke-late-edit/`, plus bulk `POST /…/bulk-grant-late-edit/` `{plan_ids, granted_until}` and `…/bulk-revoke-late-edit/` `{plan_ids}` (both return `{updated, results[]}`, unknown IDs silently skipped). 403 for director and everyone else on all four. `reason` is **optional** on every grant variant (stored as `""` when absent — kept on the model for future audit display but never required on input). Serializer surfaces `late_edit_active` (computed) + `late_edit_granted_by_name` for UI display. Frontend: new `GrantExtensionModal` (DatePicker with showTime + `disabledDate`; no reason field), single **toolbar buttons** "Open editing for all blocks" / "Close editing for all blocks" (admin-only) that operate on every plan visible in the current week — replaces the originally-shipped per-row buttons after feedback: admins want one click for the whole week, not per-block. Warning `Alert` banner above the grid lists blocks under active extension; error toast surfaces the server's "Plan edits closed" message to managers who hit the wall. Toast/UX uses `sonner` and `plan.*` namespace (~14 keys × 3 locales). 24 backend tests in `tests/test_late_edit_extension.py` covering window helper boundaries (Sat noon, Sun 23:59:59, Mon 00:01, passive expiry), service integration (manager allowed before / blocked after / allowed-during-extension, admin always allowed, actual_value untouched), per-row endpoints (admin-only, past-datetime rejected, optional empty reason accepted, persists fields, revoke clears), and bulk endpoints (admin-only, applies to all listed plans, unknown IDs skipped silently, past datetime / empty plan_ids rejected, revoke clears all). Type-check + backend tests clean. **Deploy step: run the `greenhouse` migration so `late_edit_granted_*` columns exist.** (`docs/obsidian/processes/weekly-harvest-planning.md`, `reference/api-endpoint-map.md`, `reference/data-model-map.md` updated.)
- **Soft delete (deactivate) for shipments** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). A third, reversible removal path distinct from `cancel` (lifecycle transition with a required reason) and `bulk-delete` (permanent, cascade). New `Shipment.deleted_at` + `deleted_by` (migration `0025`) act as a Gmail-trash flag — soft-deleted rows are hidden from **every** list, sheet, and board queryset by default. Two admin-only actions: `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/{id}/soft-delete/` and `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/{id}/restore/`, both idempotent, both write an `AuditLog` row (`action='soft_delete'` / `'restore'`); editing a deleted row via `partial_update` returns 403 (mirroring the archived-row guard). Admins find them on the Shipments page via a new `?show_deleted=true` checkbox — the list flips to show **only** deleted rows (with a `deleted_by_name` field surfaced on the serializer) and the per-row Edit button is replaced by a Restore (`UndoOutlined`) button. The Sheet column header (every shipment is a column) gained a small admin-only trash icon at the bottom-right (the seq number sits top-left and the color-dot top-right, so bottom-right is the one free corner) that fires the same soft-delete with a confirm modal — hidden during reorder mode. Detail-style actions (cancel, transition, restore, retrieve) bypass the soft-delete filter so admins can target a deleted row by ID, but list-style endpoints (list, sheet, board) all filter it out; the dashboard's `active_shipments` panel also excludes them. 12 new `shipment_soft_delete.*` + 2 `shipments.show_deleted` / `chip_show_deleted` i18n keys × 3 locales. **Deploy step: run the `export` migration so `deleted_at`/`deleted_by` exist.** Type-check + `python manage.py check` clean.
- **Admin bulk hard-delete on Shipment list** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). New `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/bulk-delete/` `{ids:[…]}` (admin role or superuser only — tighter than `cancel`'s `PRIVILEGED_ROLES`), capped at 200 IDs/call, wrapped in `transaction.atomic`. Bypasses the operational/archive filter so admins can purge by ID regardless of view. Cascade removes the shipment's comments, status_log, firm_splits, block_sources, pallets, quality, sales_report, custom_field_values, advance_links. Draft `QuotaUsageRecord` rows are deleted (mirrors the `cancel` cleanup); approved ones are left orphaned (`shipment_id=NULL` via the existing `SET_NULL`) and their IDs are returned in `approved_quota_to_reconcile` so the admin can reconcile via QuotaUsageGrid. One `AuditLog` row per shipment with `action='delete'` is written before destruction (AuditLog's `object_id` is a plain `IntegerField`, not an FK, so all prior update/transition rows survive). Frontend: `ShipmentList` bulk action bar gained a red **Delete permanently** button (visible only when `user.is_superuser || user.role === 'admin'`), with an antd `Modal.confirm` warning about the cascade; row selection is now also enabled for admins even on the archive view (so historical shipments can be purged). 6 new `shipment_bulk.delete_*` i18n keys × 3 locales. `docs/obsidian/reference/api-endpoint-map.md` updated. Type-check + `python manage.py check` clean.
- **Sheet column reorder mode — global drag-to-reorder** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). A **Reorder columns** toolbar button (visible only to `admin` / `export_manager` / superusers) toggles a mode where the shipment **column headers** can be dragged left/right to set a **global** order every user sees. While the mode is on, cell editing is locked (`SheetCell` short-circuits click-to-edit) and the per-row reorder/hide controls are disabled so the two reorder mechanisms can't conflict; frozen shipment columns are temporarily released (effective `shipmentFreezeCount = 0`) so all columns drag in one track. Dragging uses **dnd-kit** (`useSortable` headers in a `DndContext`/`SortableContext`, `horizontalListSortingStrategy`, `autoScroll` so edge-dragging scrolls the virtualized track); `SortableContext` holds all shipment IDs though virtualization renders a subset. On drop, `arrayMove` sets an optimistic `sheetStore.columnOrder` (ShipmentSheet's `filtered` useMemo reorders by it, tolerant of stale/new IDs) and `useSaveSheetColumnOrder` POSTs the full id list; the optimistic order clears after the next server refetch (guarded by `!reorderMode`). Backend: new `Shipment.sheet_position` (`PositiveIntegerField`, null, indexed; migration `0024`); the `/sheet/` queryset now orders `F('sheet_position').asc(nulls_first=True), '-date', '-id'` — manually-placed columns in saved order, brand-new (NULL) shipments at the top by date (NULLS FIRST = newest, where `-date` would place them). New `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/sheet-order/` `{shipment_ids:[…]}` → `{updated:N}`, server-gated to `is_superuser || role ∈ {admin, export_manager}` (narrower than `PRIVILEGED_ROLES` — excludes `director` — matching the button), validates list-of-ints + rejects duplicates, assigns sparse `(idx+1)*1024` via `bulk_update(batch_size=500)`. 5 i18n keys (`sheet.reorder_columns*`) × 3 locales. Type-check clean; backend migration check clean. (`shipment-sheet.md` updated.) **Deploy step: run the `export` migration so `sheet_position` exists.**
- **Two-column Join shipment creation** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). A second, coexisting way to create a shipment that mirrors the old Google-Sheet manual process — built directly in the Sheet (where each shipment is a column). **Soltanmyrat (`loading_dept_head`, packaging & loading)** opens a *supply column* via a "New supply shipment" toolbar button → `SupplyDraftModal` (no destination), saved as a `draft` with `skip_forecast_check=true` so it is exempt from the 18,500 kg one-truck cap (a supply column aggregates a day's harvest and may span more than one truck — it's split/joined downstream); the forecast-first one-truck draft path keeps both caps. The modal mirrors the DraftPool composer: blocks are picked from the **forecast/harvest pool** (`useHarvestForecastRemaining`) and each kg field **defaults to the block's whole remaining harvest but is editable** (take less than the whole block, or trim a >18,500 amount), uncapped; an "X kg available" hint shows each block's remaining; requires a forecast entered for the date. The blocks still count toward the forecast remaining-pool's `allocated_kg`, so the forecast-first flow is unaffected. `loading_dept_head` was newly granted draft-create permission (previously warehouse_chief + privileged only). **Gadam (`export_manager`)** opens a *destination column* via "New destination shipment" → `DestinationDraftModal` (country + import_firm + customer + optional firm_splits, no blocks). Gadam then clicks **Join** to arm a column-selection mode, clicks two draft columns directly in the Sheet (highlighted; the `JoinActionBar` auto-detects which is the destination/target vs supply/source and joins immediately on click — no confirmation dialog): `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/{target_id}/join/` `{source_id}` moves the source's block_sources (and firm_splits if target has none) onto the target, copies `variety`/`export_code` where the target's are empty, recomputes `weight_net`, writes one audit `ShipmentStatusLog` on the target, notifies the source's creator, and **hard-deletes** the source. Target stays `draft` (assigned via the existing `/assign/` flow). Gates: caller privileged; both `draft`; target≠source; target has country+customer and no blocks; source has ≥1 block. Draft-create (`is_draft=true`) extended to accept `varieties[]` (1–4 sorts — a shipment can carry multiple tomato varieties, reusing the existing `Shipment.varieties_dominant` M2M; first = primary FK, `variety_confidence='low'`), `import_firm`, `firm_splits[]`, and `skip_forecast_check`; the join copies the source's `varieties_dominant` onto a target that has none. Sheet items now expose `created_by_role` (used to tint supply columns — manual `column_color` takes precedence) and `varieties_dominant` (the variety cell shows all sorts joined when >1). The `SupplyDraftModal` uses a multi-select `VarietySelect` and a pool-driven whole-block picker (above). New `ExportFirmSelect`/`ImportFirmSelect` self-fetching controls; `sheet.new_supply`/`new_destination`/`join_bar.*` i18n × 3 locales. 34 backend tests in `tests_shipment_join.py`; type-check clean. (Docs: `draft-shipments.md`, `shipment-creation.md`, `shipment-sheet.md`, `api-endpoint-map.md`, role docs updated.)
- **Forecast-First Draft Flow** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). Replaces split-at-assignment with a harvest-forecast pool that drives one-truck drafts. New `GET /api/v1/export/harvest-forecast/remaining/` (per-block remaining = forecast − already-drafted) and `POST /api/v1/export/harvest-forecast/` (upsert `HarvestDayEntry.forecast_value` per block) in `views_harvest_forecast.py`, backed by `services/harvest_forecast.py` (`get_remaining_for_date`, `assert_draw_within_pool` — both strip `.order_by()` before subquery wrapping for MSSQL). Draft creation now validates each block's draw stays within the remaining pool. The `/assign/` endpoint promotes a draft to **`gumruk_girish`** (state-machine v2), not `yuklenme` (v1). Frontend: new `ForecastEntryModal` (enter today/tomorrow forecast per block), `DraftComposerModal` shows per-block available kg drawn from the pool, `DraftPool`/`AssignmentBoard` updated. Removed the old split UI: `SplitTrucksPanel`, `ExportFirmSelect`, `ImportFirmSelect`. `forecast.*` + new `draft.*` i18n keys × 3 locales. Tests in `tests_harvest_forecast.py`; `tests_draft_promote.py` updated for the v2 promote target. (Follow-ups noted in review: vestigial `harvest-forecast/remaining/` URL alias, forecast-POST per-block `WeeklyHarvestPlan` get_or_create N+1, GET role-gate vs docstring.)
- **Shipment Sheet fullscreen mode** (feat(frontend)). The Sheet toolbar (right side, next to Comments) gained a ⛶ Fullscreen button. It enters a distraction-free view where the page pins itself over the whole viewport (`.sheet-page--fullscreen`: `position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:1000`), covering the AppLayout sidebar (z-index 100) and header (z-index 99). The toolbar is **unmounted** — only the grid plus a small floating circular exit button (top-right) remain; exit via that button or the **Esc** key. State: `sheetFullscreen` in `sheetStore` (`setSheetFullscreen`/`toggleSheetFullscreen`), ephemeral (not persisted — a per-session view choice) and force-reset on page unmount so navigating away can't leave the flag stuck. 2 new i18n keys (`sheet.fullscreen_enter/exit`) × 3 locales. Type-check clean. (`shipment-sheet.md` updated.)
- **Shipment Sheet zoom controls** (feat(frontend)). The Sheet toolbar gained a `−` / `%` / `+` zoom group (next to the Settings gear): clamp 60 %–150 % in 10 % steps, click the `%` to reset to 100 %, persisted per browser (`localStorage ygt-sheet-zoom`). Because the Sheet is virtualized, CSS `zoom`/`transform` on the scroll container would desync `scrollLeft` from `getBoundingClientRect` and silently break `@tanstack/react-virtual` — instead a new `scaleSheetLayout(zoom)` helper (`constants/sheetRowConfig.ts`) multiplies every layout px (column widths, row height, frozen-band offsets) so the virtualizer's `estimateSize` and the rendered widths stay in lockstep (`SheetGrid`, `SheetCell`, `SheetCellEditor`, `SheetLabelColumn` all read `sheetZoom` and scale identically; column virtualizer `.measure()` re-runs on zoom change). Fonts + cell padding scale via a `--sheet-zoom` CSS variable set on `.sheet-grid` (`calc(Npx * var(--sheet-zoom))`). Store: `sheetZoom` + `zoomIn`/`zoomOut`/`resetZoom`/`setSheetZoom` in `sheetStore`. 3 new i18n keys (`sheet.zoom_in/out/reset`) × 3 locales. Type-check + sheet tests clean.
- **Shipment cancellation — 14th terminal status** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). A `cancelled` status (`step_order=99`, `phase='CANCELLED'`, no trigger field) reachable from any non-terminal status via a dedicated `POST /api/v1/export/shipments/{id}/cancel/` action with a **required** free-text reason. `export_manager`/`director` only. Cancelling bulk-sets open/in-progress/blocked tasks to `CANCELLED`, preserves comments, deletes draft `QuotaUsageRecord` rows and reports approved ones (`draft_quota_deleted`, `approved_quota_to_reconcile`) for manual reconciliation. Cancelled shipments are hidden from the operational list by default (`?show_cancelled=true` / `?status_code=cancelled` reveals them; detail pages stay reachable) and never appear on the Kanban board. Frontend: red Cancel button + reason modal in `ShipmentDetailHero`, cancelled-state `RouteTimelineRail`, red `StatusTag`, `useCancelShipment` hook, `show_cancelled` list filter, i18n × 3. Also fixes a latent `get_allowed_transitions` crash on `barysh_gumrugi` 3-tuple edges. 27 tests in `tests_cancel.py`. See ADR-019; `shipment-lifecycle.md` updated.
- **ShipmentList column manager with Sheet-parity fields** (feat(p3) + feat(frontend)). The Shipments list table gained a column-settings gear (ProTable `ColumnSetting`): show/hide any column, drag to reorder, pin left/right, with the layout persisted per browser via `columnsState` (localStorage `ygt.shipmentList.columnsState.v2` — versioned suffix so adding columns can't leave a stale stored layout showing them all). To make the picker useful, `ShipmentListSerializer` was expanded to return **all scalar shipment fields** (Sheet parity — AD-1/operator timestamps, weight detail, transport, doc checkboxes, per-role notes, vehicle condition, refs, audit); nested `firm_splits`/`block_sources` stay detail/sheet-only. The list queryset adds `select_related('import_firm', 'created_by', 'quality')` to stay N+1-safe. Seven columns remain visible by default; the rest are opt-in via `HIDDEN_BY_DEFAULT`. ~40 new column titles × 3 locales. `IShipmentListItem` extended; `IShipmentDetail.has_peregruz` override removed (now inherited as required `boolean`); list/overdue/detail mocks updated. Type-check clean. (Updates `api-contract.md` list-shape note + `shipment-list-vs-sheet.md`.)

### Fixed
- **Sheet row triggers admin: couldn't clear row color** (fix(frontend)). The `SheetRowStylePopover` `ColorPicker` sent the alpha channel (`#RRGGBBAA`, 9 chars) when the user touched opacity, blowing the backend `style_color CharField(max_length=7)` with `Ensure this field has no more than 7 characters.`; on top of that, the clear path went through `onChange` → `toHexString()` (no `null`), so the red-slash icon couldn't actually clear. Switched to `onChangeComplete` (commit-only — no per-tick PATCH burst), added `disabledAlpha` so the hex stays `#RRGGBB`, defensive `.slice(0, 7)` in case an older Ant build still emits alpha, added an explicit `onClear → style_color: ''` (model field is `blank=True` non-nullable, so empty string is the correct cleared value — sending `null` returned `This field may not be null.`), and dropped the `'#000000' → null` hack that prevented users from picking black. Mirrors the working pattern in `SheetColumnHeader`. Type-check clean.
- **Sheet: Soltanmyrad (loading_dept_head) couldn't edit his cells** (fix(p3)). The previous fix (commit `b71f746`) only added missing `SheetRowRoleTrigger` rows, but `can_edit_sheet_field` AND-composes that trigger gate with the per-role `RoleFieldPermission` allowlist — and `loading_dept_head` was missing the second-gate grant for 5 of his default rows: `block_sources` (R8), `loading_started_at` (R19), `loading_ended_at` (R20), `rejected_weight_kg` (R34), `harvest_date` (R39). His deputies (`warehouse_chief`) already had `loading_started_at`/`loading_ended_at`/`harvest_date`, so the head was strictly less capable than his deputies despite the seed-permissions comment promising parity. New core migration `0013_grant_loading_dept_head_sheet_fields` adds the 5 missing `RoleFieldPermission` rows via `get_or_create` (idempotent, additive) and clears `dynamic_perms:all_fields:loading_dept_head` + `:fields:loading_dept_head:shipment` from the cache so live workers pick the change up without a restart. `seed_permissions.py` `FIELD_DEFAULTS` was also updated so fresh DB seeds get the right perms. `shipment_code` is intentionally NOT granted — it's auto-generated server-side and the row is `is_visible=False` on the sheet. 29 sheet-permission tests pass. **Deploy step: run `python manage.py migrate core` to apply 0013.**
- **Shipment Sheet: variety cell showed code instead of name** (fix(frontend)). In `getCellValue.ts` the `'variety'` cell's multi-variety branch (`varieties_dominant.length > 1`) joined each sort's `code`, so columns carrying more than one tomato sort rendered registry codes (e.g. `MARW, 01`) instead of the readable names. Now it maps `v.name`, matching the single-variety branch which already returned `variety_name`. The backend `ShipmentSheetSerializer` already supplies both `name` and `code`, so no backend change was needed. Type-check clean.
- **Shipment Sheet: horizontal scroll cost trimmed further** (perf(frontend)). Three small follow-ups to the per-cell-Tooltip removal. (1) `.sheet-cell` got `contain: layout paint` so a mounting cell can't invalidate its siblings' or the row's layout/paint during the per-tick virtualizer rebuild. (2) The row-accent styling that ran through `.sheet-row:has(.sheet-cell--<style>) .sheet-label-col--field` was replaced with direct modifier classes (`sheet-label-field--<style>`, `sheet-label-who--<style>`) applied in `SheetLabelColumn` from `rowConfig.style` — the `:has()` selector was being re-evaluated by the browser on every cell mount/unmount inside the row. (3) Column virtualizer `overscan` bumped 5 → 8 so a sweep crosses the mount-boundary less often (fewer mount bursts per scroll). No behavior change.
- **Shipment Sheet: horizontal scroll janky — cells lagged behind the scrollbar** (fix(frontend)). The grid virtualizes the column axis, so every horizontal scroll step **mounts a fresh batch of cells** (~15 visible columns × ~60 rows ≈ 900 cells). Two antd `<Tooltip>` components sat on that per-cell mount path: `SheetCell`'s default branch wrapped every cell in a `<Tooltip>` (truncation hint, shown only when text > 15 chars), and `CommentMarker` rendered **another** `<Tooltip>` around the hover-hint icon for every cell with **zero comments** (~95% of a fresh sheet, each also calling `useTranslation()`). An antd Tooltip is `rc-tooltip` + `rc-trigger` + a portal + alignment observers — mounting ~1,800 of them while scrolling dropped frames and left cells trailing the scroll position. Fix: both tooltips removed from the hot path. The truncation hint is now a browser-native `title` attribute on the cell `<div>` (same `value.length > 15` condition, zero React overhead); the comment hover-hint drops its tooltip entirely (the chat icon is self-explanatory — no information loss). No behavior change otherwise; the unused `comments.add_to_cell` i18n key is now dead but harmless. Type-check clean. (`shipment-sheet.md` unaffected — internal perf only.)
- **Sheet multi-select dropdowns hard to close** (fix(frontend)). The `firm_splits` (R9) and `block_sources` (R8) multi-select cell editors save & close only on click-outside (`onOpenChange`), so to dismiss one the operator had to click another cell — which immediately opened *that* cell's editor, an accidental edit cascade. Added an explicit **Done** button to the multi-select dropdown (`dropdownRender` footer) that commits the selection and closes in place; its `onMouseDown` is prevented so clicking it doesn't blur-then-double-fire the outside-click path, and a `multiCommittedRef` guard makes the save idempotent if both fire. Click-outside still works unchanged. New `sheet.multiselect_done` i18n key × 3 locales. Type-check clean. (`shipment-sheet.md` updated.)
- **Whole-app performance pass: bundle weight + global polling** (fix(frontend)). Follow-up to the Sheet fixes, from a production-build + audit sweep of the whole app. **(1) `xlsx` lazy-loaded** — `ShipmentList` did `import * as XLSX from 'xlsx'` at module top, so SheetJS (~95 kB gzip) shipped in the list page chunk and downloaded on every visit even when nobody exported. Moved to `const XLSX = await import('xlsx')` inside the export handler → **ShipmentList chunk 103 kB → 8.6 kB gzip**. (New `shipments.export_failed` toast on failure, × 3 locales.) **(2) echarts tree-shaken** — `EChart.tsx` imported the full `echarts-for-react`/`echarts` (~1 MB) into the lazy BossDashboard chunk; switched to `echarts/core` + `echarts-for-react/lib/core`, registering only the modules our charts use (`LineChart`, `TooltipComponent`, `LegendComponent`, `GridComponent`, `CanvasRenderer`) → **BossDashboard chunk 387 kB → 191 kB gzip**. (Add a module to `echarts.use([...])` if a future chart renders blank.) **(3) global polling halved** — `useNotifications` and `useMyTasks` (both mounted app-wide via `AppLayout`) polled every 30 s; raised to 60 s (v5 already pauses the interval while the tab is backgrounded). Also removed an unrelated unused-import build break in `SupplyDraftModal`/leftover. Type-check clean; production build verified.
- **Comments list N+1 + uncached quota dashboard** (fix(p3)). Two backend findings from the same audit. **(1)** `CommentSerializer.get_mentions_users` ran `User.objects.filter(id__in=…)` **per comment** (N+1 across a 50-row page; `mentions_ids` is parsed from comment text, so it can't be `prefetch_related`'d). Added `CommentViewSet.paginate_queryset` → `_build_mention_users_map` which resolves the union of all mentioned user ids for the page in **one** query and hands a `{id: chip}` map to the serializer via `get_serializer_context`; the serializer uses it when present and falls back to a single-object query otherwise (detail/create responses stay correct). Chip building refactored into reusable `CommentSerializer.user_chip`. **(2)** `QuotaDashboardView` ran several aggregation passes per request **uncached** (unlike dashboard_summary/boss/KPI); added a 60 s cache keyed by `season:product_type:date_from:date_to`, mirroring the established pattern (`product_type` lowercased so casing variants don't double-cache). `python manage.py check` clean. **⚠ Backend tests NOT run — run `python manage.py test apps.export` before merging** (the test runner needs the MSSQL test DB and an interactive confirmation that couldn't be answered here).
- **Shipment Sheet re-render storm — sheet & app felt slow even with ~20 shipments** (fix(frontend)). The Sheet's hot-path components (`SheetCell`, `SheetGrid`, `SheetCellEditor`, and the `ShipmentSheet` page) subscribed to the Zustand `sheetStore` with a **bare `useSheetStore()`** — no selector — so any store change (selecting a cell via `setActiveCell`, typing in the toolbar search, toggling the comments drawer, zoom) re-rendered the *entire* grid. Because `SheetCell` read the store directly, its surrounding `memo()` was bypassed, so a single cell click re-rendered all ~800–1400 visible cells, each re-running `getCellValue` (dayjs parsing). This — not data volume — is why clicking a cell, opening a dropdown, and typing in the editor lagged at only ~20 shipments. Fix: convert every Sheet store read to a **granular selector** — setters are selected individually (stable refs, never trigger re-renders) and each `SheetCell` now derives its own active state as a primitive boolean (`s.activeCell?.shipmentId === id && …`), so selecting a cell re-renders only the two cells whose active state actually flips, not the whole grid. No behavior change; type-check clean. (Remaining deferred scaling fix for 2000+ shipments: backend `/sheet/` pagination/caching.)
- **Shipment Sheet: every cell edit refetched the entire un-paginated season** (fix(frontend)). `useShipmentPatch.onSettled` invalidated the broad `['shipments']` key, which prefix-matches `['shipments','sheet']` — so each cell save triggered a full refetch of the whole-season sheet payload (and `SheetCellEditor`'s explicit re-invalidations compounded it). The optimistic update already showed the typed value instantly, making the refetch pure overhead except for server-computed side effects (an auto-advanced status + its AD-1 timestamps via `auto_advance_if_ready()`, a recomputed `total_amount_usd`). Fix: on PATCH success, **reconcile the sheet + list caches from the response** via `mergeServerScalars` — folds only the response's *scalar* values for keys already on the cached row, so it captures those side effects while ignoring the detail serializer's differently-shaped nested fields (`firm_splits`/`block_sources`/`quality`) and never introducing detail-only keys. `onSettled` now invalidates everything under `['shipments']` **except the sheet** (predicate `queryKey[1] !== 'sheet'`) plus the single-row `['shipment',id]` detail and `['my-tasks']`; board/list are inactive on the sheet page so this only marks them stale (no network). Same treatment applied to `useShipmentPatchMulti` (edit drawers). The rarer nested-data edits (custom fields, firm-split/block-source junctions) keep their explicit `['shipments','sheet']` invalidation — they genuinely need the refetch and aren't the per-cell hot path. `mergeServerScalars` exported + covered by 5 new unit tests (scalar override, server-null, skip objects/arrays, skip absent keys, preserve omitted row fields); 10 tests in `useShipmentPatch.test.ts`, type-check clean.
- **Shipment Board invisible for every non-superuser + Permissions page missing pages** (fix(p3) + fix(frontend)). The Shipment Board (Kanban) menu item gates on `canSeePage('/export/shipments/board')`, but that route was missing from `ROUTE_PAGE_MAP`, so it resolved to a page_code no role had → invisible for export_manager and everyone else. Root fix: register every navigable page so it is matrix-configurable. Added 8 page_codes to `PAGE_REGISTRY` (`export.shipments.board`, `me.board`, `director.stuck_shipments`, `audit_log`, `feedback.submit`/`my_tickets`/`public`/`admin_inbox`) + matching `ROUTE_PAGE_MAP` entries, and converted the six items that were hardcoded-role-gated (Stuck Shipments, Audit Log, 4 Feedback pages, My Tasks) to page-permission gating in `AppLayout.tsx` + `App.tsx` (removed the `FeedbackAdminGate` wrapper and the `/admin/feedback` inline role check — the inbox is now `feedback.admin_inbox`, seeded admin-only; a superuser now sees it, consistent with every other page). `seed_permissions.py` PAGE_DEFAULTS updated to preserve current visibility exactly (board defaults visible to every role that sees `export.shipments`; `me.board`+feedback common visible to all; stuck/audit/inbox unchanged). **`audit_log` is intentionally not `admin.audit_log`** — director/export_manager must keep it, and their defaults strip all `admin.*` (AD-15). **Deploy step: run `python manage.py seed_permissions` (no `--reset`) to insert the new rows — without it these pages fail-closed.** AD-15 page-default tests pass; `docs/obsidian/processes/permissions-system.md` updated.
- **ShipmentList status filter never filtered** (fix(frontend)). The Shipments list "Status" dropdown sent status *codes* (`tamamlandy`, plus stale values `planlanyan`/`satylyor` that no longer exist) as the `?phase=` param, but the backend filters `status__phase=<value>` against `ShipmentStatusType.phase` (`DRAFT/CUSTOMS/LOADING/TRANSIT/BORDER/SALES/COMPLETE`) — so the filter never matched and selecting a status had no effect. `PHASE_KEYS` and the `phases.*` labels (×3 locales) are now keyed to the real DB phase values, in workflow order. (The previously-reported untranslated `shipment.detail.supervisor_view` / `tasks.trigger_*` keys were already fixed in c9c6905 — they resolve once a stale deploy updates. The search box was verified correct — controlled `Input.Search`, no change needed.)
- **Task auto-close: stale Task `target_fields` snapshots reconciled** (fix(p3)). `Task` rows snapshot `target_fields`/`completion_rule`/`target_value` from their `TaskRule` at creation time; editing a rule left existing open tasks watching the old field (e.g. `assign_driver` still watching `driver_id` after the rule moved to `driver_name`), so they never auto-closed even when the work was done. New `reconcile_open_tasks_with_rules()` (`services/task_rules.py`) + `manage.py reconcile_tasks` (`--dry-run`/`--shipment`) re-syncs open tasks to their current rule and re-resolves; `seed_task_rules` runs it after upserting rules so the drift cannot recur. Applied to live DB (3 tasks synced, 1 auto-closed). 18 tests in `tests_task_reconcile.py`.

### Changed
- **Weekly Plan: Sunday column hidden by default** (feat(frontend)). The Sunday (7th) day-column widened the grid and is rarely used, so it's now hidden by default with a Show/Hide Sunday toolbar toggle (`uiStore.planShowSunday`). The toggle drives both the harvest grid and the truck-allocation table (new `showSunday` prop on `TruckAllocationTable`). Sunday is always last in `DAYS`, so slicing it off preserves every other day's `di` index (date offsets, `day_of_week`). Display-only — backend still creates day-entry rows for all 7 days. 2 i18n keys (`plan.show_sunday/hide_sunday`) × 3 locales.
- **Self Board task drawer fully editable inline** (feat(frontend)). The drawer now drives off the clicked task instead of `shipment.my_task` (which is null for supervisor roles), so both assignees and supervisors can complete a task without leaving the board. The task's target fields and the other shipment fields render through the shared Sheet cell renderer (`SheetCellEditor` + `getCellValue` extracted from `SheetCell`), fixing previously non-editable `driver_name`/timestamp task fields and raw-ID / `[object Object]` display in the field list. System + official shipment codes shown at the top; `['my-tasks']` invalidated on field save so auto-closed cards drop off the board. New `docs/obsidian/reference/task-rules.md` (full task list: responsible role, completion method, opening status) + `screens/self-board.md`.

### Added
- **Main dashboard summary endpoint** `GET /api/v1/export/dashboard/summary/` (feat(p3)). New `DashboardViewSet` (`views_dashboard.py`) and pure-aggregation service `dashboard_summary.py`. Returns season, six stat cards (total/in_transit/selling/completed/no_report/quota_firms), alerts (overdue reports, quota exceeded, docs pending, weekly plan), per-country route breakdown with top-4 cities, and up to 5 live active shipments. Permission: `IsAuthenticated` only — no role gate. 60 s server-side cache (`'dashboard:summary'`). Weekly plan alert uses `HarvestDayEntry.plan_value` (not the dropped `*_plan_kg` wide columns on `WeeklyHarvestPlan`). 11 tests in `tests_dashboard_summary.py` — all pass.

### Changed
- **Export → Shipment Dashboard: removed the urgency sidebar + made the detail drawer editable** (feat(frontend)). Per beta feedback the `UrgencyPanel` left sidebar (which only surfaced a "Missing Reports" card next to the list) was dropped from `ShipmentDashboard.tsx`; the shipment list now spans full width and the missing-reports count stays in `DashboardHeader`. The `.dashboard-urgency` CSS rule was removed.
