The `DetailSlide` drawer — previously read-only — gains an **Edit** button in its header that opens the shared, permission-aware `ShipmentEditDrawer` against the already-loaded `IShipmentDetail`, so users can edit key fields inline (single multi-field PATCH via `useShipmentPatchMulti`, `['shipment']` invalidation refreshes the slide). No new i18n keys (reuses `common.edit`). Type-check clean.
- **Draft composer "Shipment Code" made readable** (fix(frontend)). Beta feedback: the Draft/Multi-block Composer modal was "hard to understand." Three fixes in `OfficialCodeEditor.tsx` + `DraftComposerModal.tsx`: (1) the cryptic `Combined: 21|MY|||26|` preview — the raw pipe-delimited storage format leaking into the UI, where empty fields looked like `|||` punctuation — is replaced by labelled per-field slots (Day · Month · Seq · Block · Year), each its own box with a placeholder dash when empty so blanks read as "to fill", not as separators; (2) "Export Code" no longer appears three times (always-on banner + header tag + preview tag) — the dual-code explainer moved behind a `?` popover on the "Shipment Code" title, and the Export Code is shown once in the preview row paired with the assembled code; (3) the Variety dropdown was **dropped from the draft UI** (per Finding #3 — no morning variety breakdown; matches the existing "variety recorded at packaging" notice) while the stored `export_code` keeps all 6 `|`-separated fields with the variety slot empty (backend validator still requires exactly 6). Cramped/truncated labels ("Seq this mon…", "Block/sub…") shortened to single words with `?` tooltips and stacked above their inputs (vertical Form layout) so nothing clips. 4 new i18n keys (`official_code.field_seq_hint`, `field_block_hint`, plus shortened `field_seq`/`field_block`) × 3 locales. Type-check clean.
- **My Work Board promoted into the Export sidebar group + drawer surfaces full shipment context** (feat(frontend)). Operational roles live on `/me/board`, but it was buried at the bottom of the sidebar in its own one-item `me.nav.label` group below Export / Management / System. Moved the menu item to sit right after the Sheet item inside the Export group (`AppLayout.tsx` lines 147–164); the badge count, icon, label key (`me.nav.board`), and the all-roles list stay byte-identical so visibility doesn't change. The standalone `me.nav.label` group entry was deleted. Final Export order: Shipments → Sheet → **My Work Board** → Dashboard → Board → Drafts → Assign → Overdue → Advances. **Drawer enrichment** (`SelfBoardTaskDrawer.tsx`): the drawer is now the single completion surface. Body order is task fields at the top (`<MyTaskCard>` unchanged — same target-field editor, auto-save per field, Mark Done button), then a `<Collapse>` panel "Other shipment details" (new i18n key `me.board.drawer_more_details`, **collapsed by default**) holding read-only `<Descriptions column={1} size="small">` blocks for every `EDIT_FIELD_GROUPS` section (Logistics, Transport, Goods, Finance, Status, Notes). Two filters keep the section compact: fields that are this task's targets are skipped (no duplication with the editor above), and any field whose formatted value is null/empty is dropped (groups that end up empty are skipped entirely). Values come from the existing `IShipmentDetail` payload via a small `formatShipmentFieldValue` helper — FK selects use the `_name`/`_display` partner (`country_name`, `customer_name`, `vehicle_responsible_display`, etc.), numbers carry the editor's `suffix` (`18,500 kg`, `3 d`, `4 °C`), dates format via dayjs, booleans render as `common.yes`/`common.no`. **No FieldEditor instances, no `useShipmentPatchMulti`, no Save button** — non-task fields are read-only context only; users edit them on the Shipment Sheet or Shipment Detail page exactly like before. The prominent footer button "Open shipment detail" was demoted to a small de-emphasized link at the bottom of the drawer body (kept as an escape hatch for the read-only status log / comments / route timeline that aren't in the drawer). Done/cancelled cards still render only `<ReadOnlyTaskSummary>` (no "Other details" section underneath — confirms `isActiveCard` guard). 1 new i18n key × 3 locales; type-check, lint, 30 tests clean.

### Added
- **Restored ESLint v9 and cleared all 28 pre-existing warnings** (chore(frontend)). New flat `frontend/eslint.config.js` with `@typescript-eslint`, `react-hooks`, `react-refresh` plugins; dropped the deprecated `--ext` flag from the lint script (incompatible with flat config). `npm run lint` now exits zero with `--max-warnings 0` enforced. To clear the 28 warnings the linter surfaced on its first clean run, extracted helpers from 6 component files into sibling `*.helpers.ts` files (HarvestCell, SheetCellEditor, PhaseContextStrip, TaskCardEditor, QuotaIssuancesList, ShipmentDetailHelpers) so `react-refresh/only-export-components` is satisfied without breaking HMR; wrapped 13 unstable `data?.foo ?? []` references in `useMemo` across ImportFirmsPage, PermissionsPage (3x), StuckShipments, OverdueReports, QuotaDashboard, ShipmentSheet (2x), WeeklyPlanGrid, SelfBoard, SheetCellEditor; fixed misc hook-dep warnings (ScreenshotInput ref-cleanup copy-to-local pattern, CommentComposer + MentionPopover `useCallback` declared before keyboard-nav `useEffect` to avoid TDZ, SheetCell drop unused `navigate` dep). 13 vitest tests, `tsc --noEmit`, and `vite build` all clean.
- **EChart wrapper accessibility props + BlocksHeatmap keyboard a11y** (feat(frontend)). New `ariaLabel` and `decorative` props on `<EChart>`: content charts get `role="img" + aria-label`, sparklines next to KPI numbers get `aria-hidden`. RevenueChart passes a descriptive aria-label ("Revenue by week, current versus previous season"); HeroKpiStrip marks each sparkline `decorative`. BlocksHeatmap rewritten for keyboard/SR users: outer grid `role="group"` + aria-label, each tile becomes `role="button"` with `tabIndex={0}` and Enter/Space handlers (previously mouse-only); tile aria-label and tooltip both come from a single `boss_dashboard.heatmap_tile_aria` key that includes block / actual kg / plan kg / pct AND the band name (so colour is no longer the only signal for colour-blind users). 11 new i18n keys × 3 locales for the chart description, heatmap description, parameterised tile string, and 5 band names (excellent/good/ok/warn/alert).
- **`docs/UI_UX_AUDIT.md`** — single-file audit report covering tests, ESLint state, i18n violations, table-component inconsistency, component-size offenders, hardcoded colours, chart/heatmap a11y, and keyboard accessibility gaps. Tracks the punch list this branch worked through.

### Added
- **Vitest coverage for LoginPage and ProtectedRoute** (test(frontend)). The UI audit's biggest test-coverage gap was the auth boundary — both files had zero tests despite gating every page in the app. New `LoginPage.test.tsx` (5 tests): renders the title + both inputs + submit button, blocks the API call and surfaces both required-field errors on empty submit, posts credentials and navigates to `/` for non-boss roles, navigates to `/boss/dashboard` for boss role, does not navigate when the API rejects. New `ProtectedRoute.test.tsx` (12 tests): spinner during `isLoading`, redirect to `/login` on `isError` or null user, role allow-list match renders children, role miss redirects to `/unauthorized`, superuser bypasses both role and pageCode gates, `pageCode` string match grants, missing pageCode redirects, array `pageCode` ORs (any match grants, none redirects), and parent-page inherits child-page permission (`export.quota.local_sell` grants `export.quota`). `useAuth` and `@/services/api` are mocked at module level; `useNavigate` is a `vi.fn` while the rest of `react-router-dom` stays real (so `MemoryRouter` + `Routes` still render). Total suite is now 30 tests across 5 files (was 13/3). `tsc --noEmit` and `npm run lint --max-warnings 0` clean.

### Changed
- **Font usage consolidated behind `FONT` constants** (refactor(frontend)). The UI audit flagged inconsistent font handling: 28 components wrote `fontFamily: 'monospace'` inline (system mono), one wrote `fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, monospace)'` (CSS var), `App.tsx` hardcoded the full `'DM Sans', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif` stack as a token, and only a handful used the existing `FONT.mono` export from `constants/styles.ts`. Result: shipment codes / variety codes / monospace numerics rendered in two different fonts across the app depending on which file you opened. Single-pass migration replaces every `fontFamily: 'monospace'` and `var(--font-mono, monospace)` reference with `FONT.mono` (= `'JetBrains Mono', monospace`); the App.tsx `ConfigProvider` token now uses `FONT.default`. 53 substitutions across 30 files; auto-merged with existing `COLORS` imports from `@/constants/styles` (4 files added a new import). The CSS vars `--font` / `--font-mono` in `src/index.css:3-4` were already defined and loaded via `main.tsx` — the audit's "var not defined globally" claim was stale; the real fix was making JS/TSX usage consistent with what CSS was already providing. No visual regression in components already on JetBrains Mono; components previously on system mono now match. Lint, type-check, 13 tests all pass.
- **All data tables migrated from `mantine-datatable` to Ant Design `ProTable`** (refactor(p3,admin,frontend)). The "all tables use ProTable" rule in `frontend-arch.md` was violated by 10 pages and one bare `Table`; brought all of them onto ProTable. Migrated: TruckForecast, BlockSummary, OverdueReports, PricePanel (was native Mantine `Table`, restructured as a city × dates pivot row model), AuditLogPage, SeasonsPage, DomesticSales, DashboardPage, UsersPage, AdvancesTracker (the largest at 473L, includes row-expansion → `expandable.expandedRowRender` and the nested LinkedShipments sub-table). Each migration adds `sorter` on every meaningful column with `defaultSortOrder` where natural (date columns descend, name/code columns ascend, reference lists with `is_active` use active-first + secondary sort); `search={false}`, `options={false}`, and `pagination={false}` (or `{ pageSize: 50 }` for lists that grow) match the original UX. Form-heavy pages (SeasonsPage, UsersPage, AdvancesTracker) also swap `@mantine/form` `useForm` for Ant `Form.useForm` with `onFinish`; `Mantine Modal` (opened/onClose) → Ant `Modal` (open/onCancel) with `destroyOnClose`. Bonus fixes caught during the work: 3 more hardcoded strings (`overdue.subtitle`, `prices.subtitle`, `domestic_sales.subtitle` were rendered in Turkmen for every UI language) wrapped in `t()` and added to all three locales.
- **Frontend split: 4 oversized pages broken into sibling-component dirs** (refactor(p3,feedback,frontend)). `AssignmentBoard.tsx` (709 → 286L) → `assignment/SupplyCard` + `DemandCard` + `MatchPanel` + `assignmentHelpers`. `PalletManifest.tsx` (613 → 194L) → `pallet/ManifestStats` + `DistributionPills` + `VarietyRollupCard` + `PalletTable` + `palletHelpers`. `AdminInboxPage.tsx` (483 → 75L) → `inbox/TicketCard` + `TicketListPanel` + `TicketReplyThread` + `TicketDetailPanel`; the page itself is now just the responsive shell (mobile drawer vs desktop two-pane). `AppLayout.tsx` (581 → 477L) extracts `NotificationBell` to its own shared component (KIND_COLOR moves with it). Page-level state, mutations, navigation stay in the orchestrator; presentation moves to siblings. No behaviour change.
- **i18n: 13 hardcoded user-visible strings wrapped in `t()`** (fix(frontend)). The audit flagged strings rendered in one language regardless of UI locale: `AdvancesTracker` batch_code placeholder, `LocalSellPlanGrid` "Double-click to edit" tooltip, `AuditLogPage` model + object_id filter placeholders, `SheetRowsTab` 4 custom-row example placeholders, `StatusesTab` role + phase placeholders, `OptionListsTab` code + icon placeholders, `ReportsGrid` Excel + PDF button labels, `HeroKpiStrip`/`DebtBreakdown`/`FirmRiskMatrix` "Demo" tag (shared `common.demo_badge` key), `AppLayout` sidebar "YGT Platform" brand text. New keys live in existing namespaces (advances, local_sell, audit_log, sheet_rows, shipment_settings, boss_dashboard.reports, common, nav); for technical example placeholders (`kz_remarks`, `ADV-2026-XXX`, `Excel`, `PDF`) the value is intentionally identical across all three languages.
- **A11y: 7 icon-only buttons gained `aria-label`** (fix(p3)). Grid/list view toggle in QuotaUsageTab (high-traffic), week prev/next in WeeklyPlanGrid + QuotaUsageGrid, month prev/next in QuotaUsageGrid, back arrow in AddQuotaIssuance + PalletManifest. 5 new i18n keys (`plan.prev_week`/`next_week`, `quota_usage.prev_month`/`next_month`/`view_grid`/`view_list`) + reuse of existing `common.back`. Side-fix during step 6: AdminInbox filter Selects (status / category / author) also gained `aria-label`.

### Removed
- **Mantine entirely removed once all tables migrated** (chore(frontend)). After the 10 ProTable migrations no component or page imports anything from `@mantine/*` or `mantine-datatable`. Removed: `MantineProvider`, `DatesProvider`, `createTheme`, and the Mantine theme object (Card / Button / Modal / NavLink defaults extensions) from `App.tsx`; Mantine CSS imports from `main.tsx`; the `mantine-datatable` forwardRef-warning suppressor and the Vite-HMR portal-cleanup that scrubbed orphan Mantine backdrops (neither relevant anymore). Uninstalled 7 npm packages: `@mantine/core`, `@mantine/dates`, `@mantine/form`, `@mantine/hooks`, `@mantine/notifications`, `mantine-datatable`, plus the now-orphaned `postcss-preset-mantine` + `postcss-simple-vars` + `postcss` devDeps (no `postcss.config.*` in the repo). Build artifact: main index chunk shrinks ~1393 KB → ~1249 KB (gzip 446 → 400 KB).

### Changed
- **/me/board tasks now solvable inline via a side drawer** (feat(frontend)). Clicking a kanban card on the Self Board no longer navigates to `/shipments/{id}`. A right-side drawer (`SelfBoardTaskDrawer`) opens and reuses the existing `MyTaskCard` (target-field editor, progress bar, Mark Done button, overdue styling) by fetching the shipment via `useShipmentDetail`. Done/cancelled cards open a read-only summary instead. Drawer footer has an "Open shipment detail" link as an escape hatch. Drag-to-block / drag-to-unblock untouched — `draggedRef` still suppresses click after drag. New `SelfKanbanCard` `onCardClick` prop; absent it falls back to the old `navigate(...)` behaviour. Two new i18n keys (`me.board.drawer_open_shipment`, `me.board.drawer_readonly_completed`) added to tk/ru/en. No backend changes.

- **Shipment state machine v2 — restructured + auto-advance on field fill** (feat(p3)). The 13-step lifecycle becomes a 12-step machine: yuklenme (Loading) moves from step 1 to step 3 (after both customs steps, matching operational reality — paperwork pre-cleared while truck loads). `serhet_tm + serhet_gechdi → serhet_gechdi` (single "Crossed TM Border"). `hasabat + tamamlandy → tamamlandy` (single "Report received & Completed"). Two new statuses: `dest_entry` (Destination Entry, fires when Sheet R31 fills; the UI label appends the country name) and `transshipment` (only inserted in the chain for `has_peregruz=True` shipments — for non-peregruz, `barysh_gumrugi → bardy` direct). `satyldy` renamed to "Sold (waiting for Report)". Retired: `serhet_tm`, `yolda`, `hasabat` (kept in DB at step_order 100+ with `is_active=False` for audit reference). New `is_active` column on `ShipmentStatusType`. Auto-advance: when every non-MANUAL_DONE Task for the current step is DONE, `Shipment.save() → auto_advance_if_ready()` fires `transition_to(next_code, is_auto=True)` — bypasses the role gate, credits `shipment.updated_by`, marks the status log row `is_auto=True`. Re-entry guarded with a thread-local flag — strictly one step per outer save. Customs Entry triggers on `documents_status == 'in_progress'` via a new `FIELD_EQUALS` `TaskCompletionRule` + `target_value` column on `TaskRule`/`Task`. `TRANSITIONS` tuple shape extended to optional 3rd predicate element for the peregruz fork at `barysh_gumrugi`. `create_shipment()` now starts at `code='draft'` (was step_order=1). `/assign/` endpoint promotes `draft → gumruk_girish` (was `→ yuklenme`). `set_sales_report` gate moved from step_order≥12 to step_order≥11 (satyldy). Migrations: `core.0009_add_status_type_is_active`, `core.0010_state_machine_v2`, `export.0021_remap_retired_statuses` (one-shot data migration: `serhet_tm → serhet_gechdi`, `yolda → barysh_gumrugi`, `hasabat → tamamlandy`, each with a `is_manual_override=True` audit log row), `export.0022_add_status_log_is_auto`, `export.0023_task_field_equals`. `seed_task_rules` rewritten with one auto-resolving trigger rule per step (21 active rules; legacy MANUAL_DONE rules retained as operational reminders, hasabat rules deactivated). Frontend: `RouteTimelineRail` switches from a hardcoded 13-step list to a v2 12-step list that conditionally splices `transshipment` when `has_peregruz=True`; `dest_entry` cell appends the country name; status log rows with `is_auto=True` render an "Auto" Tag. i18n: new keys `shipment_status.{draft,dest_entry,transshipment,auto_badge}` plus updated labels for `satyldy` and `tamamlandy` in tk/ru/en. New `IShipmentDetail.has_peregruz` field. Tests: new `tests_auto_advance.py` (6 tests covering FIELD_EQUALS, peregruz fork, re-entry guard, no-rules guard). Existing `TransitionServiceTest` rewritten for v2 chain (12 tests, plus 2 new ones for `is_auto` flag + role bypass). All new tests pass. Frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean.

### Fixed
- **Sheet R31 dest_entry_at cell rendered as `'—'` even after a save** (fix(p3)). The field was wired through the model / serializer / patch flow but missing from `SheetCell.getCellValue`'s `tsFields` array, so the formatter fell through to the default `'—'`. Added `'dest_entry_at'` to the timestamp list — cell now renders `DD.MM HH:mm` like the other datetime rows.

### Changed
- **Sheet R25 (TM Customs Exit / customs_exit_at) flipped from AD-1 readonly to operator-entered datetime** (feat(p3)). The last remaining AD-1-locked cell. Şirin (`document_team`) now logs the customs-exit time directly on the Sheet instead of getting it auto-stamped at the `transition_to('gumruk_chykysh')` moment. `STATUS_TIMESTAMP_MAP` is now empty — AD-1 is functionally retired; `transition_to()` still updates `status` + `status_changed_at`, but no longer stamps any lifecycle column. `customs_exit_at` added to `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']`, `seed_permissions['document_team']`. `ShipmentAdmin.readonly_fields` shrinks to `created_at + updated_at` (audit-only). Deleted `test_ad1_timestamp_rejected_via_patch` — the contract it asserted (some lifecycle field rejects PATCH) no longer applies to any column. Model docstring and ADR-010 amendment updated to "AD-1 retired (final)". All 13 date/datetime sheet rows now save round-trip cleanly. 58 tests pass.

### Changed
- **Sheet R30/R32/R35/R41/R42 flipped from AD-1 readonly to operator-entered datetime** (feat(p3)). border_crossed_at (TM Border Exit), customs_entry_at (Destination Customs), arrived_at (Arrival), sale_started_at (Sale Start), sale_ended_at (Sale End) now behave like R19/R21 — operators pick the time on the Sheet. Practical reason: warehouse/transport/sales staff fill these from physical events (gate stamp, sale concluded) that don't line up with the workflow's status transitions; they need to enter the actual time, not get whatever `timezone.now()` was at the moment they clicked "Next status". `STATUS_TIMESTAMP_MAP` now contains a single entry (`'gumruk_chykysh' → 'customs_exit_at'`); all five removed mappings mean `transition_to('serhet_gechdi')`, `transition_to('bardy')`, etc. no longer stamp their target field. Five fields added to `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']`. `seed_permissions`: `transport` gains `border_crossed_at` (R30); `sales_rep` gains `customs_entry_at`/`arrived_at`/`sale_started_at`/`sale_ended_at` (R32/R35/R41/R42). `ShipmentAdmin.readonly_fields` shrinks to `customs_exit_at + created_at + updated_at`. `test_ad1_timestamp_rejected_via_patch` switched from `customs_entry_at` to `customs_exit_at` (the only remaining AD-1 field). Existing rows keep their values; new shipments transitioning past these statuses now leave the timestamps null until an operator fills the cell. ADR-010 amendment updated — AD-1's single-write-path invariant applies to one column instead of eight. Downstream readers (`boss_analytics.loaded_today` reads `loading_started_at`, no change needed; transit-day calculations on `departed_at`/`arrived_at` will now see null until operators fill them). R25 (TM Customs Exit, customs_exit_at) intentionally left as the last AD-1-locked readonly cell — user did not request the change. 59 tests pass.

### Changed
- **Sheet R7 Export Code row hidden by default** (chore(p3)). The `shipment_code` row in the Sheet body was a read-only duplicate of what `SheetColumnHeader` already shows at the top of every shipment column. Migration `export.0016_hide_shipment_code_sheet_row` flips `SheetRowSetting.is_visible=False` (with `hidden_at=now()`) for `field_key='shipment_code'`. The /sheet/ endpoint now excludes it from the payload for every user; the column-header Export Code is unchanged. Reversible — the migration's `reverse_code` restores `is_visible=True` / `hidden_at=None`, and an admin can also re-show it through the SheetRowSetting admin once that UI lands. Existing `ShipmentComment` threads anchored to `field_key='shipment_code'` are unaffected.

### Added
- **Sheet R47 Görnüşi (shipment type) row** (feat(p3)). New row toggles `Shipment.is_gapy_satys` directly from the Sheet — values "Adaty" (False, default) and "Gapy Satyş" (True). Two-option dropdown styled like `has_peregruz`: value `0`/`1` on the wire, mapped to `false`/`true` by a new `isBoolDropdown` branch in `SheetCellEditor` (refactored from the previous `isPeregruz`-specific check so the same code path now handles both bool-backed dropdowns). New `case 'is_gapy_satys'` in `SheetCell.getCellValue` returns the translated label so the cell never shows `—`. Flipping a row to Gapy Satyş immediately blanks all `gapy_hidden=True` cells (border_point, customs_*, dest_*) for that shipment via the existing client-side `isGapy` check. Editing gated by `can_edit_field(role, 'is_gapy_satys')`; defaults grant only `admin` / `director` / `export_manager` (via wildcard `RoleFieldPermission`). Row added at `row_number=47` — same pattern as `export_code` (R46) and `customs_clearance_planned_day` (R45); admin can pin a logical position via `SheetRowSetting.display_order`. New i18n keys `sheet.row.gornushi` + `sheet.gornushi.{adaty,gapy_satys}` in tk/ru/en. No backend serializer / migration changes (field already in `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS` and `ShipmentSheetSerializer.Meta.fields`).
- **Per-shipment column tint in the Shipment Sheet** (feat(p3)). Admin + export_manager (and director / superuser via existing wildcard `RoleFieldPermission`) can now flag individual shipment columns with a colour to make important / unusual trucks pop in the Sheet view. New `Shipment.column_color` field — nullable `CharField(max_length=7)` holding `#RRGGBB`. Wired into `ShipmentSheetSerializer.Meta.fields`, `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, and `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']` (no seed change — admin / export_manager / director already grant `'*'` on `shipment`). Frontend: new `SheetColumnHeader` component renders a tiny picker dot in the top-right of every column header (hidden for roles without edit access); selection triggers `useShipmentPatch` (optimistic update inherits column_color via the existing `{ ...old, ...fields }` spread). Visual: tinted cell backgrounds via `color-mix(in srgb, var(--col-tint) 18%, var(--surface))` so text stays readable; the header itself gets a 3px top border in the raw colour. New i18n keys `sheet.column_color.{tooltip,presets}` in tk/ru/en. Migration `export.0014_add_shipment_column_color`. Each colour change writes one `AuditLog` row through the existing sheet-PATCH audit hook. Obsidian doc `screens/shipment-sheet.md` updated. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `makemigrations --check` clean.

### Changed
- **Sheet R17/R18 converted from comment-count cells to freeform text fields** (feat(p3)). Notes (Soltanmyrat) and Notes (Şirin) now behave like Gadam's `export_manager_note` (R5) — plain text inputs that persist to the shipment row. Two new `Shipment.warehouse_note` and `Shipment.document_note` `TextField` columns added (cyrillic collation, blank/default `''`). Sheet rows R17/R18 flip from `input_type='comment_count'` to `'text'`; their `field_key` changes from `warehouse_comment_count`/`document_comment_count` to `warehouse_note`/`document_note`. The viewset's `Count('comments', filter=Q(...))` annotations and the matching `IntegerField` serializer fields are removed (single sheet query is leaner — one less per-row aggregate). Migration `export.0013_add_warehouse_document_notes` adds both columns and demotes any orphan `ShipmentComment.field_key='warehouse_comment_count'/'document_comment_count'` to NULL so existing comment threads survive as shipment-level. Permissions: `warehouse_note` editable by `loading_dept_head` + `warehouse_chief` (mirrors the shared-fields pattern in `seed_permissions`); `document_note` editable by `document_team`. Frontend updates: `IShipmentSheetItem` / `IShipmentDetail` swap the count fields for the two new note strings; `SheetCell` drops the `isCommentCount` branch + chat-bubble rendering and routes the new field keys through the existing text fallback (so values render plainly and double-click opens the same `Input` editor used by `export_manager_note`). Mock data, services/comments.py `SHEET_FIELD_KEYS`, runbook, and obsidian docs updated. Per-cell `CommentMarker` is unaffected — anyone can still leave threaded comments on R17/R18 cells.

### Added
- **Sheet R39 supports per-block harvest dates** (feat(p3)). Multi-block trucks can have tomatoes harvested on different days; a single shipment-level date doesn't capture that. Added `ShipmentBlockSource.harvest_date = DateField(null=True, blank=True)` (migration `export.0018_add_blocksource_harvest_date`) as the primary source, with the existing `Shipment.harvest_date` retained as a fallback for single-block trucks or unset rows. R39 cell now reads `block_sources[].harvest_date`, sorts ascending, and renders as a min–max range (`01.05–03.05`); single-date and one-block cases collapse to one date. The cell editor flips from a basic DatePicker to a `Popover` containing one DatePicker per block plus a shipment-level fallback picker — Save dispatches `POST /shipments/{id}/block-sources/` (per-block dates) and `PATCH /shipments/{id}/` (shipment fallback) in sequence. Backend `set_block_sources` view now reads existing `block_id → harvest_date` map before deleting rows so R8's multiselect editor (which only sends `block_id`) doesn't silently reset the dates. `harvest_date` added to `SheetBlockSourceInlineSerializer` + `DraftBlockSourceInlineSerializer`, `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment_block_source']`, `IBlockSource` + `ISheetBlockSource` types, mocks. Three new i18n keys (`sheet.harvest_date.{no_blocks,shipment_fallback,save,cancel}`) in tk/ru/en. 59 tests + `tsc --noEmit` clean. Browser smoke test not run in this session.

### Fixed
- **Sheet R39 (Harvest Date) was an unwritable orphan cell** (fix(p3)). The row had `input_type='readonly'` and pointed at a `harvest_date` field that never existed on `Shipment` — it just rendered `'—'` for every shipment with no way to edit. Same fix shape as R43: added `Shipment.harvest_date = DateField(null=True, blank=True)` (migration `export.0017_add_harvest_date`), flipped R39 to `'date'`, wired through `ShipmentSheetSerializer.Meta.fields`, `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']`, `seed_permissions['warehouse_chief']` (Soltanmyrat owns R39 per `default_who_key`), `IShipmentSheetItem` + `IShipmentDetail` types. `SheetCell` `dateOnlyFields` list now includes `'harvest_date'` so it formats as `DD.MM.YYYY`. Per-block `ShipmentBlockSource.harvest_date` (the spec alternative for multi-block trucks) deferred — Sheet has one cell per shipment column, so a shipment-level field matches the layout. Audit confirms all six date/datetime rows (R19/R20/R21/R34/R39/R43) save round-trip cleanly. 59 tests pass.
- **Sheet R43 (Report Date) save was silently dropped** (fix(p3)). Same root cause as R20 — the row's `field_key` was `has_sales_report` (a derived boolean annotation, EXISTS over the `sales_reports` table), not a stored column on `Shipment`. `input_type='date'` rendered an Ant DatePicker on double-click, the PATCH succeeded with HTTP 200, but `ShipmentPatchSerializer.Meta.fields` filtered the unknown field out and nothing persisted. Added a real `Shipment.sales_report_date = DateField(null=True, blank=True)` (migration `export.0015_add_sales_report_date`) and re-pointed R43 at it. `has_sales_report` stays untouched as the read-only ✓/❌ boolean used by other call sites (Detail page, freshness badges). Wired through `ShipmentSheetSerializer.Meta.fields`, `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']`, `seed_permissions['sales_rep']` (Aganazar's row), `IShipmentSheetItem` / `IShipmentDetail` types. Added a date-only branch in `SheetCell.getCellValue` (formats `DD.MM.YYYY` instead of the `DD.MM HH:mm` used for datetime fields). Audit confirms all five date/datetime sheet rows (R19/R20/R21/R34/R43) now save round-trip cleanly. 59 tests pass.
- **Sheet R20 `loading_ended_at` save was silently dropped** (fix(p3)). The field had been removed from the `Shipment` model during the migration collapse but never re-added — the row was still listed in `sheet_rows.py` (R20, `input_type='datetime'`) and in `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']` / `seed_permissions['warehouse_chief']`, so the cell rendered an editor and accepted a date pick, but the PATCH body was stripped by `ShipmentPatchSerializer.Meta.fields` and nothing persisted. Re-added the field as a plain operator-entered `DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)` (NOT AD-1 — no transition writes it). Migration `export.0012_add_loading_ended_at`. Field added to `ShipmentSheetSerializer.Meta.fields`, `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, and the SheetCell timestamps list (so it formats as `DD.MM HH:mm`). 41 sheet + draft tests still pass.

### Changed
- **Sheet R21 `departed_at` becomes operator-entered; no longer written by transition_to** (feat(p3)). Same treatment as R19/R20 — warehouse staff picks the greenhouse-departure time directly on the Sheet. Row 21 flips from `input_type='readonly'` to `'datetime'`; `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS` and `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']` gain `departed_at`; `seed_permissions` adds it to `warehouse_chief`; `STATUS_TIMESTAMP_MAP` no longer maps `'yola_chykdy'` (so `transition_to('yola_chykdy')` leaves the field null); `ShipmentAdmin.readonly_fields` drops it. Existing rows keep their values; new shipments transitioning to `yola_chykdy` will have `departed_at=null` until an operator fills the cell. `test_ad1_timestamp_rejected_via_patch` switched to assert `customs_entry_at` (still AD-1) instead of `departed_at`. Six AD-1 timestamps remain transition-only.
- **Sheet R19 `loading_started_at` becomes operator-entered; not auto-set on create** (feat(p3)). The truck-loading start time is now a regular sheet cell that warehouse staff fill in, not an AD-1 transition stamp. Sheet row 19 flips from `input_type='readonly'` to `'datetime'`; `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS` and `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']` gain `loading_started_at`; `seed_permissions` adds it to the `warehouse_chief` editable set; `STATUS_TIMESTAMP_MAP` no longer maps `'yuklenme'` (so `transition_to('yuklenme')` and the draft→yuklenme assign action both leave the field null); `create_shipment` stops calling `_write_ad1_timestamp` for it; `ShipmentAdmin.readonly_fields` drops it. Partial deviation from ADR-010 (AD-1) — the other seven lifecycle timestamps still go through `transition_to()` only. Existing rows keep their values; new shipments start with `loading_started_at=null` until an operator picks a date in the sheet. Boss analytics' "loaded today" filter and the daily harvest rollup will only see shipments that have a value entered.
- **Sheet datetime picker shows hour:minute only (no seconds)** (fix(p3)). `SheetCellEditor`'s `'datetime'` editor now renders with `showTime={{ format: 'HH:mm' }}`, displays `DD.MM.YYYY HH:mm`, and zeroes seconds before saving (`date.startOf('minute').toISOString()`). Operators were never supposed to track seconds for loading/peregruz timestamps; the default `showTime` exposed an unused field.

### Removed
- **Weekly-level "Submit week" step on the harvest plan grid** (refactor(p3)). The week-level submission button was vestigial after the approval workflow was retired (AD-15) and per-cell saves became the only write path — `set_plan_value` already stamps `plan_submitted_at` / `plan_submitted_by` / `plan_state` on every blur, so calling `submit_weekly_plan` only back-filled rows for legacy/imported data and set a container-level `WeeklyHarvestPlan.submitted_at` that no UI or job consumed. Full clean-up: (1) backend — service `apps/greenhouse/services/submit_plan.py` deleted, `submit_week` action removed from `WeeklyHarvestPlanViewSet`, `submitted_at` / `submitted_by` fields dropped from `WeeklyHarvestPlan` model + serializer, migration `greenhouse.0004_drop_weeklyharvestplan_submitted_fields` removes the two columns. (2) Dispatcher — P1/P2/P3 plan-missing triggers (`_compute_plan_trigger`) now check "any HarvestDayEntry for this block in the plan week has `plan_value IS NOT NULL`" instead of `WeeklyHarvestPlan.submitted_at IS NOT NULL`; semantics: as soon as the manager has entered any cell, we stop nudging. (3) Frontend — `useSubmitHarvestPlan` hook deleted, Submit button / "Submitted" status tag / Actions column / `handleSubmitPlan` / `isCurrentOrFutureWeek` derived flag removed from `WeeklyPlanGrid`, `submitted_at` / `submitted_by_name` removed from `IWeeklyHarvestPlan` and the mock fixture, eight `plan.*` i18n keys retired (`submit`, `status_submitted`, `actions`, `pending_approval`, `rejected_note`, `toast_submitted`, `toast_approved`, `toast_rejected`) across en/ru/tk. (4) Obsidian doc updated. 35 greenhouse backend tests still pass; `tsc --noEmit` clean.

- **Legacy KanbanBoard page (`/export/kanban`) deleted in favour of ShipmentBoard (`/export/shipments/board`)** (chore(frontend)). The old page-code (`export.kanban`) and route remained in App.tsx, AppLayout sidebar, `permissions.ts` route map, and `pathLabels.ts` long after `ShipmentBoard` (which uses `pageCode="export.shipments"`) became the active board. Removed: `frontend/src/pages/export/KanbanBoard.tsx`, the `KanbanBoard` lazy import + Route, the `/export/kanban` sidebar item + ROUTE_LABELS entry, the `/export/kanban → export.kanban` map entry in `permissions.ts`, the `/export/kanban → nav.kanban` entry in `pathLabels.ts`. Backend: dropped `('export.kanban', 'Kanban Board')` from `permission_registry.PAGE_REGISTRY`, dropped the `'export.kanban'` page from the role-page sets in `seed_permissions.py` (loading_dept_head, warehouse_chief, document_team, transport, sales_rep), and dropped `'export.kanban'` from the operational-pages list in `tests_permission_matrix.py`. Existing `RolePagePermission` rows with `page_code='export.kanban'` remain in the DB as orphans and can be cleaned up later via a data migration. Shared kanban components (`KanbanColumn`, `ShipmentKanbanCard`, `SelfKanbanCard`) and the `kanban.days_stuck` i18n key are still used by `ShipmentBoard` / `SelfBoard` / `ShipmentCardAnt` and were left untouched. Other docs that still mention KanbanBoard (CHANGELOG history, ADR, SPRINT_PLAN, ONBOARDING, TESTABLE_FEATURES, frontend-dev agent) intentionally untouched — historical references are accurate as of their dates.

### Fixed
- **Sheet: import_firm cell stayed blank after selection** (fix(p3)). `ShipmentSheetSerializer.import_firm_name` was sourced from `import_firm.name_en`, but `ImportFirm` has no `name_en` column (only `name_company` and `name_short`), so `default=None` swallowed the AttributeError and the API always returned `null`. After the editor saved a valid `import_firm` id the patch succeeded but the rerendered cell still resolved to `'—'`. Replaced the field with a `SerializerMethodField` returning `firm.name_short or firm.name_company`, matching the editor's display rule (`f.name_short ?? f.name_company`). No other call site referenced `import_firm.name_en`.

- **Weekly Plan grid: Fri/Sat cells silently truncated by default page size** (fix(p3)). `useDayEntries` was hitting `/greenhouse/day-entries/?…` without a `page_size`, so DRF's `StandardPagination` capped the response at 50 rows. With 15 blocks × 6 operating days = 90 entries per week, the first 50 (sorted by `entry_date, block__code`) covered Mon–Wed in full plus part of Thu, and Fri/Sat fell off the page entirely — they rendered as `—` em-dashes that looked uneditable but were actually missing. Hook now passes `page_size=200` (the backend cap) so a full week always fits in one response. Comment in the hook documents the math so it's not repeated.

- **Weekly Plan grid: single-click cell focus, Tab/Shift+Tab navigation, admin past-day overrides** (fix(p3)). Three usability fixes on `WeeklyPlanGrid`. (1) `<InputNumber>` cells in `HarvestCell` previously needed two clicks to land the cursor inside the input — `autoFocus` is unreliable inside Ant `<Table>` cells because the input mounts inside a parent that just received the synthetic click. Replaced with `useRef<ComponentRef<typeof InputNumber>>` + `useEffect` that calls `.focus({cursor:'all'})` whenever an `editingX` flag flips on. Now single-click drops the user into the input with text selected. (2) Spreadsheet-style keyboard nav extended in `tableNavigation.ts`: Tab moves right, Shift+Tab moves left, Enter still moves down, arrows still work, Escape still blurs. The new `findNextTarget` walks siblings looking for either an existing `<input>` (cell already in edit mode) OR a wrapper marked `data-edit-cell="true"` (cell in display mode); when it finds the latter it dispatches a click to enter edit mode, then focuses the freshly mounted input via two `requestAnimationFrame` ticks plus a `setTimeout(80ms)` fallback. Editable wrappers in HarvestCell (today_actual, tomorrow_forecast_input, future_plan, past_actual-for-admin) now carry the `data-edit-cell` attribute. (3) The `past_actual` cell mode previously had no edit path even for admins, contradicting the spec's "admin: anytime, any block, with required reason". Added three admin-only edit branches: clicking the actual value enters edit mode for `actual_value`; clicking the small Plan / Forecast hint rows (with `<EditOutlined>` icons) enters edit mode for `plan_value` / `forecast_value` respectively. Existing `AdminOverrideReasonModal` flow handles the reason capture; the `set_plan_value` / `set_forecast_value` / `set_actual_value` services already enforce reason-required when overriding a non-null value, so backend is unchanged. Three new i18n keys (`plan.admin_click_edit_{actual,plan,forecast}`) in tk/ru/en. TypeScript clean (`tsc --noEmit`). Browser smoke test not run in this session.

### Added
- **Weekly Plan grid edit affordances for `loading_dept_head`; computed-actual source badge** (feat(p3)). Frontend follow-up to the backend permissions / rollup commits. `WeeklyPlanGrid` permission helpers rewritten: `canEditForecastForEntry` allows `loading_dept_head` for any block when `now` is between 00:00 local day-before and 12:00 local day-of (mirrors backend `LOADING_HEAD_FORECAST_DAY_OF_CLOSE`); `canEditActualForEntry` is admin-only since actuals are now computed by the daily rollup; `warehouse_chief` removed from both paths. `HarvestCell` gains a new `today_forecast_input` display mode that surfaces the forecast input on today's cell for `loading_dept_head` before noon (admin keeps the actual-edit cell for override purposes — distinguished by `canEditActual && !canEditForecast`). New `ActualSourceBadge` component renders next to the value in `past_actual` and `today_actual` cells: small "AUTO" tag for `actual_source='shipment_rollup'`, "OR" tag for `actual_source='admin_override'`, with tooltip explaining the source. `ActualSource` type extended to `'shipment_rollup' | 'admin_override'` to match the backend choices. Fallback-mode button now visible to `loading_dept_head` instead of `warehouse_chief`. Three new i18n keys (`plan.source_shipment_rollup`, `plan.source_admin_override`) in tk/ru/en. TypeScript clean (`tsc --noEmit`); production build succeeds. Browser smoke test not run in this session — start the dev server and verify the grid renders for the loading_dept_head role before declaring shipped.

- **Daily harvest actual rollup from shipment loading data** (feat(p3)). New `rollup_actuals` management command + service computes `HarvestDayEntry.actual_value` per block per day by summing `ShipmentBlockSource.weight_kg` joined to `Shipment.loading_started_at`. Designed to run once per day on a separate cron from the 5-minute dispatcher (default schedule `30 2 * * *`); defaults to yesterday in `GreenhouseConfig.timezone_name`. Critical timezone correctness: filter uses a **timezone-aware UTC range** derived from the local target date (not Django's `__date=` lookup, which would push to the DB and evaluate in the connection's UTC and shift the answer for shipments loaded near midnight). Idempotent — re-running the same date is a no-op modulo deterministic SUM. Rows with `actual_source='admin_override'` are protected unless `--force`. Silent under-reporting surfaced: shipments with `loading_started_at` on the target date but no `ShipmentBlockSource` rows are listed in stdout and in `RollupResult.shipments_without_blocks` so ops can fill the missing block split. New service module `apps.greenhouse.services.actual_rollup`, new command `apps/greenhouse/management/commands/rollup_actuals.py` with `--date`, `--force`, `--dry-run` flags. `'shipment_rollup'` added to `actual_source` choices (`greenhouse.0003`). 6 tests cover block-sum, admin_override-skip, force-overwrite, timezone-boundary (22:00 UTC May 6 → May 7 local), silent-gap reporting, and dry-run-no-write. Live dry-run on the production-shape DB confirmed the silent-gap path is working — two shipments on 2026-05-07 are loaded without block sources.

### Changed
- **Harvest forecast now owned by `loading_dept_head` (Soltanmyrat); actuals admin-only** (feat(p3)). Soltanmyrat moved from `warehouse_chief` to `loading_dept_head` in the admin panel and now needs the Weekly Harvest Plan grid to coordinate truck loading. Backend changes: `'export.plan'` added to `loading_dept_head`'s page set in `seed_permissions.py` (data migration `core.0008` flips the existing `RolePagePermission` row from `is_visible=False` to `True` so existing DBs don't need `seed_permissions --reset`); `set_forecast_value` accepts `loading_dept_head` from 00:00 local day-before through `LOADING_HEAD_FORECAST_DAY_OF_CLOSE` (12:00 noon) on day-of, replacing the old `warehouse_chief` fallback / same-day windows entirely; `set_actual_value` is now admin-only and stamps `actual_source='admin_override'` so the upcoming daily shipment-rollup job (Commit 2) respects manual edits. `'admin_override'` added to `actual_source` choices (`greenhouse.0002`). `HARVEST_DAY_WRITE` constant updated. Obsidian doc Roles & Permissions table refreshed. Soltanmyrat can now open `/export/plan` and the grid renders; cell-level edit affordances for the new role land in the frontend follow-up commit.

### Added
- **Detail page typing UX — debounce text saves, never lock the input** (fix(p3)). Follow-up to the Detail-usable fix below: text/textarea/number inputs were saving on every keystroke and the field disabled for the in-flight PATCH, so typing whole words was impossible. Saves are now debounced 700 ms (timer reset on each keystroke; flushed when focus leaves the row) and the input is never disabled while a save is pending — the next save supersedes the in-flight one via the optimistic cache. Discrete inputs (Select, DatePicker, Switch) still save immediately. A small spinner indicates pending state without blocking input.

- **Detail page made actually usable** (fix(p3)). Four concrete bugs fixed:
  - **Sections expanded by default** — the 5 collapse panels (Logistika / Ulag / Haryt / Dokument / Maliýe) now render expanded on first load instead of all collapsed. Users see fields immediately. Manual collapse still works.
  - **Fields are inline-editable** — every editable field in every section now uses a new `<DetailFieldRow>` wrapper that pairs the existing `<FieldEditor>` (already used by Edit Drawer + MyTaskCard) with `useShipmentPatchMulti`. One source of truth for "edit a shipment field" — Sheet, Detail, and Drawer all dispatch through the same patch hook. Each row carries a stable `#detail-field-<key>` id for scroll-to.
  - **Misleading "no task" banner gone for supervisors** — `MyTaskCard` returns null when the user is `export_manager`/`boss`/`admin`/`director`. When other tasks exist for an operational role, replaced the empty-state with a softer message: "{N} tasks with other roles. You'll be notified when it's your turn." The pure empty-state ("No task assigned") only shows when truly nothing is happening.
  - **Task rows are clickable** — clicking a task in `OtherTasksRow` expands the section containing its first target field, smooth-scrolls to the field, and focuses the input. If the current user is the task's assignee_role and the task is OPEN, it also fires `POST /tasks/:id/start/`. BLOCKED tasks open a modal showing `blocked_reason` plus an Unblock button gated by role/supervisor. Hover state shows pointer cursor for affordance.
  - Backend: `TaskListSerializer` gains `blocked_reason` so the modal doesn't need a separate `/tasks/:id/` fetch per click. Additive change — 182 existing tests still pass on MSSQL.
  - Frontend: `DetailFieldRow` is a new ~75-line component; `ShipmentDetail.tsx` rewritten to map `EDIT_FIELD_GROUPS` to the 5 sections; `MyTaskCard`/`OtherTasksRow` updated; new i18n keys for the soft banner, blocked modal, and unblock button in tk/ru/en. TypeScript clean.

- **Stream G — dual-code rename: Export Code + Shipment Code** (feat(p3)). Pure relabel (no behavior change). The two Shipment code fields are now consistently labeled across the UI: `shipment_code` is shown as **Export Code** ("the code for track in our program" — auto-generated platform tracker, read-only, in the Sheet column header) and `export_code` is shown as **Shipment Code** (the operator-entered DD|MM|NNN|BLK|YY|VV pallet tag with block + variety, validated server-side, editable by warehouse_chief). Backend: new sheet row at `row_number=46` for `export_code` with `label_key='sheet.row.shipment_code'` and `input_type='text'`; the existing `shipment_code` row's `label_key` renamed to `sheet.row.export_code` (was previously `sheet.row.shipment_code`, which mislabeled shipment_code as Shipment Code). `export_code` added to `RESOURCE_FIELDS['shipment']` and to `seed_permissions` for `warehouse_chief` and `loading_dept_head`. Frontend: every i18n value of "Shipment Code" / "Код груза" / "Ýük kody" referring to `shipment_code` flipped to "Export Code" / "Экспортный код" / "Eksport kody" via JSON `replace_all`; new `sheet.row.export_code` and `shipment.detail.export_code_label` keys added to all three locales. SheetGrid drops the `shipment_code.slice(0, 7)` truncation — the full 10-char code now shows in the column header. ShipmentDetailHero replaces its single-line shipment_code display with a stacked layout: top line shows the Shipment Code (or "—"), bottom line shows "Export Code: …". DraftComposerModal's `official_code.title` and `platform_id_label` values updated transparently via i18n. 11 backend tests verify shipment_code is silently stripped from PATCH (Meta.fields filter), export_code accepts valid format / rejects bad format, the sheet payload exposes both fields, sheet row config is correct, and `can_edit_field('warehouse_chief', 'export_code')` returns True. 267 across the broader regression suite.

- **Stream F-followup — Sheet create now produces drafts; shipment_code auto-generated** (feat(p3)). Three corrections to Stream F's first cut: (1) the Sheet "+" button (`useSheetCreate`) was creating shipments at Loading regardless of Stream F's modal default — it now sends `is_draft: true` like the modal. (2) The Sheet hook used to compute a random shipment_code on the client; that's now done server-side via a new `generate_shipment_code()` helper that increments a daily sequence (`DDMMNNN/YY`) and retries on collision. The platform `shipment_code` is the auto code; Soltanmyrat's physical pallet tag lives on the separate `export_code` field, fillable later via the Sheet/Detail edit paths. (3) `ShipmentCreateModal` no longer asks for shipment_code or date — both are auto-filled by the backend (shipment_code generated, date defaults to today). Loading start time (`loading_started_at`) is no longer written at create — it's an AD-1 timestamp set by `transition_to('yuklenme')` when Soltanmyrat actually clicks "Promote to Loading". Backend serializer relaxed: `shipment_code` and `date` both optional. 4 new tests cover auto-generation uniqueness across same-day creates and that drafts never get `loading_started_at` populated. Foundational invariant: shipments NEVER start in Loading — they always start in Draft.

- **Stream F — default new shipments to draft + Promote to Loading button** (feat(p3)). The standard "New Shipment" modal now creates DRAFTS by default, exposing the prep tasks (set destination, pick export firms, assign driver, start documents prep) on the Self Kanban for the right roles. A "Skip prep" Switch on the modal opts back into the legacy yuklenme-direct path for urgent reshipments and legacy imports. Backend: `_create_draft_shipment` now calls `generate_tasks_for_status(shipment, 'draft')` after creation, so the 5–6 draft-stage tasks (one variant of `give_documents` gated by `is_gapy_satys`) appear immediately. The strict "drafts require ≥1 block source" validation was relaxed — block sources stay required for the supply-side DraftPool flow only because the DraftPool UI sends them; the standard create modal can submit empty. New `ShipmentDetailSerializer.can_promote_from_draft` boolean — true when the shipment is in draft AND every auto-resolving draft task is DONE/CANCELLED. `manual_done` draft tasks (give_documents) deliberately don't gate readiness — promotion is the user's call once automation has done what it can. New "Promote to Loading" button on the Detail hero (export_manager / director / admin only) opens a confirm modal and posts to the existing `/shipments/:id/assign/` endpoint with empty body, which transitions the draft to yuklenme via `transition_to()`. Generates the loading-stage tasks (fill_loading_data, quality_inspection) on transition. New `usePromoteFromDraft` hook invalidates shipment detail + sheet + board + drafts + my-tasks query keys so every consuming surface refreshes. 7 new backend tests; 296 across the full suite. i18n for all new strings in tk/ru/en.

- **Stream E — KPI layer + `Shipment.status_changed_at`** (feat(p3)). Final stream of the YGT P3 Tasks/Kanbans/KPI refactor. New `Shipment.status_changed_at` field is set by `transition_to()` on every status change; migration `0011_shipment_status_changed_at` backfills from the latest `ShipmentStatusLog.changed_at` per shipment (or `created_at` fallback) using `bulk_update(batch_size=500)`. D3's `time_in_phase_seconds` now reuses the existing `_resolve_phase_entry` helper from D1's detail serializer for consistent phase-boundary detection (replacing the over-resetting Max-of-status-log annotation). New `apps/export/services/kpi.py` exposes 7 helpers: `kpi_throughput` (closed/created counts in window), `kpi_cycle_time` (avg created→tamamlandy), `kpi_avg_phase_time` (per-phase residence time, cached 5min), `kpi_on_time_rate` (deadline adherence, optional role filter), `kpi_avg_task_duration` (started→completed avg, optional role filter), `kpi_stuck_shipments` (non-terminal shipments with no recent task starts), `kpi_blocked_age` (avg/max/p95 age of currently-blocked tasks). 4 new endpoints under `/api/v1/kpi/`: `dashboard/`, `by-role/?role=X`, `by-phase/`, `by-shipment/:id/` — all 60s cached. Boss Dashboard gains a Task Throughput section that reuses these helpers without rebuilding existing aggregators. 35 KPI tests + 4 backfill tests + 11 boss-endpoint smoke tests pass; 289 total across the full A1/A2/B/C/D/E suite.

- **Stream D3 — Shipment Kanban (`/export/shipments/board`)** (feat(p3)). New phase-grouped kanban board for the active season. Backend adds `GET /api/v1/export/shipments/board/` — a single bounded query (≤5 queries at 100 shipments via conditional `Count` annotations) that returns shipments grouped into the 7 phase columns (`PLAN → CLOSE`) with per-shipment task aggregates (`tasks_done`, `tasks_total`, `late_count`, `in_progress_count`, `blocked_count`) plus a `phase_avg_seconds` map for column footers (cached 5min). Filters: `country`, `customer`, `gapy_satys`, `owner_role`, `search` (shipment code icontains). Sort within column: severity-weighted late_count desc, then in_progress, then time_in_phase desc. Cards are non-draggable — status changes happen elsewhere via transitions, not by drag. Top border colour: red (any late task), amber (any blocked), blue (any in_progress), grey (idle). Click navigates to Detail. Frontend page reuses the shared `KanbanColumn` from D2; new `ShipmentKanbanCard` and `useShipmentBoard` hook (60s polling). Sidebar gains a "Board" sub-item under Shipments. 34 backend tests pass; total task+phase+board+detail_extras suite at 137. Note: `time_in_phase_seconds` currently uses the latest status-log timestamp, which over-resets when shipments cross intra-phase status boundaries (e.g. yola_chykdy→serhet_tm both in TRANSIT); will be tightened in Stream E with `Shipment.status_changed_at`.

- **Stream D2 — Self Kanban (`/me/board`) for per-role task tracking** (feat(p3)). New page that shows the current user's tasks across every shipment in 4 (or 5 with Show All) columns: To do (OPEN) → In progress (IN_PROGRESS) → Blocked (BLOCKED) → Done today (DONE where `completed_at >= midnight TM`) → History. Cards include shipment shipment code, phase tag, task title, and a deadline indicator with overdue colouring; click navigates to the new Detail page. KPI strip in the header shows today's `done_count`, `avg_duration`, and `on_time_rate` from the existing `/api/v1/me/kpi-today/` endpoint. Drag-and-drop is restricted to OPEN ↔ BLOCKED — open→blocked opens a Modal asking for `reason` and calls `/tasks/:id/block/`; blocked→in_progress calls `/tasks/:id/unblock/`. Every column accepts drops so the user gets feedback on disallowed transitions (a toast). Today-midnight boundary is anchored to `Asia/Ashgabat` via `dayjs.tz` so users on UTC-clock laptops still see their TM-day tasks correctly. Filters: phase dropdown, shipment-code search, "Show All" toggle. Polling: tasks every 30s, KPIs every 60s. Sidebar gains a new "My Work" menu group with an OPEN-task badge that refreshes on the same query cycle. New hooks: `useMyTasks`, `useMyKpiToday`. Shared `KanbanColumn` component will be reused by D3.

- **Stream D1 — task-centric Shipment Detail page rewrite** (feat(p3)). Replaces the old 4-tab UI (Overview / Document / Finance / Changes) with a single-column layout focused on the current user's task. Backend extends `ShipmentDetailSerializer` with 4 new fields: `my_task` (the requester's open task on this shipment, or null for supervisors), `other_tasks` (read-only summary of all other tasks), `in_phase_seconds` (time since the shipment entered its current phase, walking ShipmentStatusLog and respecting multi-status phases like TRANSIT), and `phase_avg_seconds` (per-status historical average across closed shipments of the active season; cached 60s; null on no history). The retrieve queryset prefetches tasks + status_log to keep the join cost bounded. Frontend ships 6 new components in `src/components/shipment/`: `ShipmentDetailHero` (cargo/status/phase/idle warning/manifest), `MyTaskCard` (only when `my_task != null`; uses `useStartTask` once-per-mount on first edit and a Mark Done button for `MANUAL_DONE` rules), `TaskCardEditor` (inline-editable target_fields), `PhaseContextStrip` (in-phase / avg-for-step / open-tasks counters), `OtherTasksRow` (compact list with state icons), `RouteTimelineRail` (extracted right-rail timeline; now indexes status_log by `status_code` rather than position to fix a bug that mismatched timestamps for partial-completion shipments). New `useTaskActions` hook wraps the 4 task action endpoints and invalidates `['shipment', id]` + `['my-tasks']` + `['shipments', 'sheet']` (the Sheet payload includes per-shipment task counts that must refresh). The 4 old tabs collapse: Overview content into `MyTaskCard` plus read-only collapsibles, Document/Finance content surfaces as task cards during their relevant statuses, Changes moves to a stub `/shipments/:id/activity` page (lazy-loaded). `ShipmentEditDrawer` is NOT deleted — `ShipmentList` still uses it for inline edit. `StatusLogSerializer` adds `status_code` so the frontend timeline can map by code. `seller` role added to `tasks.role.*` i18n in all three locales. 103 backend tests pass.

- **Stream C — canonical phase grouping for shipments** (feat(p3)). New `apps/export/services/phases.py` exposes `PHASE_MAP` (14 status codes → 6 phase codes), `PHASE_ORDER` (7 entries; `PLAN` is virtual for the kanban placeholder), `PHASE_LABELS` (i18n keys), and `get_phase()`. The status state machine in `services/shipment.py::TRANSITIONS` is unchanged — phases are a UI/reporting view over statuses. Operational order: docs prep starts in `draft` (PREP) before physical loading, so kanban order is `PLAN → PREP → DOCS → LOAD → TRANSIT → DEST → CLOSE`. `phase` field added to `ShipmentListSerializer` (and inherited by `ShipmentDetailSerializer`), `ShipmentSheetSerializer`, and `TaskListSerializer`. `select_related('shipment__status')` extended on Task/Me views to keep the join cheap. Frontend gets a literal-union `ShipmentPhase` type and a `phase.*` i18n namespace in tk/ru/en. 29 phase tests pass; 192 across the whole task + phase + A1/A2 surface.

- **Stream B-api — task REST API + Me endpoints** (feat(p3)). Third and final sub-PR of Stream B in the Tasks/Kanbans/KPI refactor. Adds the read-only `TaskViewSet` (list + retrieve) plus 5 action endpoints — `start`, `block` (requires `reason`), `unblock` (clears `blocked_reason`), `complete` (only for `MANUAL_DONE` rules), and `cancel` (admin/director only). Plus `GET /shipments/{id}/tasks/` (grouped by step), `GET /me/tasks/` (auto-filtered by the requester's role unless they're a supervisor — `export_manager`/`boss`/`admin`/`director`), and `GET /me/kpi-today/` (`done_count`, `avg_duration_seconds`, `on_time_rate`; cached 60s). New `IsTaskActor` permission gates each action: assignee-role match OR supervisor OR admin (cancel-only). List endpoint supports `?assignee_role`, `?assignee_user`, `?state`, `?shipment`, `?step`, `?overdue=true`; default order is `deadline asc, created_at asc`. `select_related('shipment', 'rule', 'assignee_user')` keeps the kanban/self-board queries bounded (assertNumQueries-tested). 46 tests pass on MSSQL; 149 total across the four task test modules. `start` from BLOCKED state is rejected with 400 — the documented recovery path is `/unblock/`, which clears the blocked_reason. Forward-compatible with Stream C: the serializer's `phase` field uses a lazy import + `'CLOSE'` fallback when `PHASE_MAP` doesn't yet exist.

- **Stream B-engine — task generation engine, deadline grammar, auto-resolution** (feat(p3)). Second sub-PR of Stream B. Ships the rule engine that powers the Task system: `parse_deadline_rule` converts grammar strings (`4h_after_status`, `13:00_same_day`, `friday_eow`, etc.) to absolute datetimes in Asia/Ashgabat TZ; `generate_tasks_for_status` idempotently creates one `Task` per matching active `TaskRule` when a shipment enters a status (called from `transition_to()` after the status log write); `resolve_for_shipment` auto-marks tasks DONE when their `completion_rule` is satisfied by current field values (called from `Shipment.save()` override — no signals, per arch rules); `mark_started_for_changed_fields` sets `started_at` + `IN_PROGRESS` on OPEN tasks whose `target_fields` intersect the fields patched in a PATCH request (called from `ShipmentViewSet.partial_update` after save). Supports dotted paths (`quality.azyk_maglumatnama`) and reverse-FK presence checks (`firm_splits`, `block_sources`). Two management commands: `seed_task_rules` (upserts 13 rules covering draft/yuklenme/gumruk_girish/gumruk_chykysh/bardy/satyldy/hasabat; idempotent, `--reset` flag) and `backfill_tasks` (processes existing shipments; `--dry-run` and `--limit N` flags). 71 tests pass on MSSQL. Docs updated in `docs/obsidian/processes/comments-tasks.md` (Structured Task Engine section). API viewsets/serializers/URLs excluded — belong to B-api sub-PR.

- **Stream B-models — `Task` + `TaskRule` Django models** (feat(p3)). Schema-only first sub-PR of Stream B in the Tasks/Kanbans/KPI refactor. `TaskRule` is a recipe — when a Shipment enters a status, the rule engine generates one Task per matching active rule (engine ships in B-engine). `Task` represents a unit of work for a specific role on a specific Shipment, with optional CSV `target_fields`, an optional deadline, and 5 states (open/in_progress/blocked/done/cancelled). Per CLAUDE.md / MSSQL, `target_fields` is a `CharField(max_length=512)` storing CSV (NOT `JSONField`); `TaskRule.condition` is two scalar columns (`condition_field`, `condition_value`) — one condition per rule, multi-condition cases use multiple rules. PKs are `BigAutoField` to match codebase idiom (UUID PKs fragment MSSQL clustered indexes). Indexes on `(shipment, state)`, `(assignee_role, state)`, `(state, deadline)` keep the kanban / self-board queries cheap. Helper properties: `Task.target_field_list`, `Task.is_overdue`, `Task.duration_seconds`. Self-referential M2M `Task.blocked_by` (symmetrical=False). Migration `0010_task_taskrule` is purely additive. 33 tests pass on MSSQL.

- **Stream A2 — `Shipment.customs_clearance_planned_day`** (feat(p3)). New CharField with weekday choices (`mon`/`tue`/`wed`/`thu`/`fri`/`sat`/`sun`), owned by `document_team` (Sirin). Records the planned weekday for customs clearance preparation. Pure additive migration `0009_add_customs_planned_day` — no data backfill. Added to permission registry, seed_permissions, sheet_rows (row 45), and the patchable serializer whitelist. Frontend renders the cell as a localized weekday dropdown via a new `weekdays` `OptionsSource`. 7 backend tests pass on MSSQL. Will be referenced by the Stream B7 `tasks.start_documents_prep` TaskRule.

- **Stream A1 — `Shipment.export_manager_note` Sheet row owned by Gadam** (feat(p3)). New `TextField` with Cyrillic collation, replacing the dropped `route_note` slot at row 5. Editable by `export_manager` and `admin` per `permission_registry`. Also seeded into `seed_permissions` for the export_manager role group. Foundational change for the Tasks/Kanbans refactor — see `.claude/plans/ygt-p3-twinkling-llama.md`.

### Removed
- **Stream A1 — dead Shipment columns: `route_note`, `customs_clearance`** (feat(p3)). Both were duplicates of existing data: `route_note` was redundant with the AD-1 lifecycle timestamps, and `customs_clearance` (a 3-state status code) duplicated `customs_entry_at`/`customs_exit_at`. Migration `0008_drop_legacy_fields_add_manager_note` runs `RemoveField` + a data-migration step that deletes the matching `SheetRowSetting` rows (also covering the orphan `cmr_status` Sheet row that had no DB column) and nullifies `field_key` on `ShipmentComment` rows that anchored to the dropped fields, preserving comment threads as shipment-level. `UserSheetRowPref` rows pointing to deleted Sheet rows cascade automatically via the existing FK. Frontend types, mocks, edit-config groups, SheetCell render arms, OptionListsTab category, and i18n keys (`shipment_detail.route_note`, `sheet.row.cmr_status`, `shipment_edit_drawer.field.{route_note,customs_clearance}`, `shipment_settings.category_customs_clearance`) cleaned up across all three locales. 6 migration tests pass on MSSQL; full apps.export suite of 17 tests still green.

- **Feedback Module v1 — centralised in-app ticketing for bugs, suggestions, and questions** (feat). Replaces the WhatsApp/Telegram/calls feedback channel with a single inbox: any authenticated user can submit a ticket with up to 5 image attachments (≤5 MB each), the admin (users with `role='admin'`) gets a sidebar badge plus an email per new ticket, and the admin can promote useful answers to a public stream readable by everyone. Spec deliberately ships narrow — no voting, no SLA timers, no auto-close, no Telegram, no user-side notifications, no shipment linking — see `docs/obsidian/feedback-module.md`.
  - **New Django app `apps.feedback`** depending only on `core`. Three models: `FeedbackTicket` (category/title/description/status/is_public + author + submitted_from_path/label + user_agent), `FeedbackReply` (mode='standard'|'internal'|'public' + denormalised `is_internal`/`is_public` synced in `save()`), `FeedbackAttachment` (FileField uploaded to `media/feedback/<YYYY>/<MM>/`, XOR check-constraint enforcing exactly one of ticket/reply parent). Cyrillic collation on every user-text CharField; no JSONField. Migration `0001_initial.py` is purely additive.
  - **API under `/api/v1/feedback/tickets/`** — list (`?scope=mine|public|all` plus `status/category/author/search/date_from/date_to/page` filters), detail (replies filtered server-side by viewer with admin → author → public → 404 fall-through precedence), POST create (multipart, validates each attachment via magic-byte check), PATCH (admin-only, `status` writable but `is_public` rejected with 400), `reopen` action (author-only, when status ∈ resolved/rejected), `reply` action (admin-only multipart; mode='public' atomically flips `ticket.is_public=true`), `admin_unread_count` action (admin-only count of `status='new'`).
  - **Email** — `services/email.py` resolves recipients = active users with `role='admin'` ∪ `FEEDBACK_ADMIN_EMAIL` env var; whole body wrapped in try/except so SMTP failure never breaks a ticket POST; dispatched via `transaction.on_commit` so a DB rollback never sends a stale email. Console backend in dev; SMTP via env vars in prod (`EMAIL_HOST/PORT/USER/PASSWORD/USE_TLS`, `DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL`, `PLATFORM_URL` for the deep link). `PLATFORM_URL` blank → email omits the URL line rather than render a broken link.
  - **File validation** — magic-byte check on first 12 bytes (PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF), 5 MB / 5 files-per-parent server-side caps, allowed-extension whitelist, `os.path.basename` to neutralise path traversal in `original_filename`.
  - **Tests (20 new cases on MSSQL, 20/20 pass)** — visibility precedence (admin sees all incl. internal; author sees own + non-internal; peer can't list or retrieve a private ticket; public ticket visible via `?scope=public`); reply-mode='public' flips `ticket.is_public`; PATCH with `is_public=true` rejected with 400; reopen by non-author returns 404 (consistent with module's "404 not 403" philosophy); bad upload (oversize / wrong magic bytes) rejected; email function returns silently with no recipients.
  - **Frontend** — four lazy-loaded routes (`/feedback/submit`, `/feedback/my-tickets`, `/feedback/public`, `/admin/feedback`) plus a fixed-position `FeedbackFAB` floating-button rendered from `AppLayout` so it appears on every authenticated page with `submitted_from_path`/`user_agent` auto-captured. `SubmitFeedbackPage` is a single-page Form with a right-side instructions panel (collapses to accordion on mobile). `MyTicketsPage` is a ProTable with a thread Drawer + Reopen action. `PublicFeedPage` is a server-paginated card list. `AdminInboxPage` is a two-pane (35%/65%) layout with status/category/author/date/search filters, a `ReplyComposer` with a three-way Radio for mode, and internal notes visually distinct (yellow background + tag). Two-pane layout collapses to list+Drawer on mobile.
  - **`ScreenshotInput` component** — four input methods: Ant Upload.Dragger (file picker + drag-drop), `paste` listener (Ctrl+V) that accumulates all clipboard images in one event, and html2canvas-based "Capture this screen" button (dynamic `import('html2canvas')` so it stays out of the main bundle). 5 MB / 5-file / image-MIME client-side validation; diff-based `useEffect` revokes orphaned `URL.createObjectURL` blobs on every files-array change to prevent leaks.
  - **Admin gate strict to `role==='admin'`** — backend permission `IsFeedbackAdmin` and frontend `FeedbackAdminGate` both check the role string only. Deliberately bypasses the project's shared `is_superuser` shortcut for `/admin/feedback` and the sidebar inbox entry, so a Django superuser whose actual role is `export_manager` cannot reach the inbox. Sidebar badge unread-count query (`useFeedbackAdminUnreadCount`) polls every 60 s and is `enabled: user?.role === 'admin'`.
  - **i18n** — `feedback.*` namespace in tk + ru + en covering nav labels, page titles, category/status names, form labels & validation, multi-paragraph instructions panel content (Win+Shift+S, Cmd+Shift+4, phone Power+Volume Down), reply-mode labels & tooltips, and toast messages. `submitted_from_label` translated at display time using a `pathLabels.ts` helper, so a Russian admin sees Russian labels for tickets submitted by Turkmen users.
  - **Out of scope (deferred to v1.1)** — voting/likes, SLA timers, priority levels, auto-close, Telegram notifications, "complaint" category, user-side notifications (no toast/bell on reply — user must visit My Tickets), structured shipment linking, audit-log entries (the reply thread is the audit trail).

- **Phase 5c of Sheet Control v2 — admin-created free-text custom rows** (feat(p3), L1 runtime per ADR-0004). Admins can now add rows to the Sheet from the Admin → Sheet Rows tab without a code change. Each row stores per-shipment free text in a child table; permission gating reuses the existing Phase 1 lock / role-trigger / extra-user machinery.
  - **Backend** — `SheetRowSetting.is_custom: BooleanField(default=False)` (read-only on the serializer; flipped only by POST create). New child model `ShipmentCustomFieldValue(shipment FK, row FK, value_text, updated_at, updated_by)` with composite UQ on `(shipment, row)`. Migration `0007_custom_rows` is purely additive: AddField (`is_custom`) + CreateModel (`ShipmentCustomFieldValue`).
  - **POST `/admin/sheet-rows/` re-enabled for custom rows.** Server enforces `field_key` starts with `custom_` (regex `^custom_[a-z0-9_]{1,53}$`), uniqueness, and at least one non-empty label. Sets `is_custom=True`, `display_order = max(existing) + 1024`, `row_number = max(100, max_row_number + 1)` so custom rows land below the legacy Excel layout (1–44) without colliding. Auto-grants the creating admin a `SheetRowUserPermission` so they can edit immediately. Writes an AuditLog row.
  - **PATCH `/shipments/{id}/custom-fields/`** — body `{field_key, value}`. `update_or_create` on `(shipment, row)`. Reuses `can_edit_sheet_field(user, field_key)` so locks / role triggers / extra-user grants from Phase 1 still gate custom rows. Empty string is allowed (= "explicitly cleared by user"; updated_at/by track the clearing). Blocks PATCH on archived shipments. Writes an AuditLog row.
  - **`/sheet/` payload extended.** Each shipment dict gains `custom_fields: {field_key: value}` (one extra grouped query, no N+1). The `rows` array synthesizes IRowConfig-shaped entries for every visible `is_custom=True` SheetRowSetting (input_type='text', default_who_key='sheet.who.custom') alongside the DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS-backed entries. Order resolution honors per-user `UserSheetRowPref.position` for custom rows the same way it does for default rows.
  - **DELETE bypasses the 30-day cooldown for custom rows.** The cooldown protects DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS from accidental nukes; custom rows are admin-owned at runtime and admins can recall them immediately. Default-row delete behavior unchanged.
  - **Frontend** — `IShipmentSheetItem.custom_fields?: Record<string, string>` and `ISheetRowSetting.is_custom: boolean`. `SheetCell.getCellValue` branches on `field_key.startsWith('custom_')` and reads from `shipment.custom_fields`. `SheetCellEditor` has a new `customFieldMutation` that PATCHes to the dedicated endpoint when the field is custom; `currentValue` resolution also branches. The Sheet Rows admin tab gains an **Add custom row** primary button + Modal with: slug input (auto-prefixed `custom_`, regex-validated client + server side) and three-language label inputs. Custom rows render with a small purple "custom" tag in the field_key column so admins can spot them.
  - **i18n** — `sheet_rows.add_custom_row`, `add_custom_hint`, `custom_badge`, `custom_modal_title`, `custom_modal_ok`, plus 8 form/error keys; `sheet.who.custom` fallback for the "Who" column when no per-row override is set. All in tk + ru + en parity.
  - **Tests (12 new in tests_custom_rows.py — 76/76 sheet-related tests pass on MSSQL)** — POST creates a custom row + auto-grants the creator; rejects non-`custom_` prefix, invalid characters, empty labels, duplicate field_keys; PATCH `update_or_creates` and is idempotent; empty-string PATCH persists as "cleared"; rejects unknown / non-custom field_keys; `/sheet/` payload includes `custom_fields`; DELETE skips the 30-day cooldown for custom rows.
  - **Out of scope** — typed custom rows (numeric, date, dropdown). Free text only; admins who need typed fields go through the L2 runbook (Phase 5b). Cross-shipment validation, formulas, and bulk import of custom values are deferred.

- **Phase 5b of Sheet Control v2 — runbook for adding typed Sheet fields** (docs(p3), L2 path per ADR-0004). New file `docs/runbooks/add_sheet_row.md` formalizes the existing developer process for adding a new column to the Shipment model + Sheet view. Covers: pick the right Django field type with the project's MSSQL gotchas (DecimalField for money, `**cyrillic_collation()` spread, string FK refs across apps, `db_column` for serializer-rename fields), `makemigrations`, `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS`, `SheetCell.getCellValue` switch arm, `SheetCellEditor` render arm, `IShipmentSheetItem` + `IShipmentDetail` types, i18n in all three locales, and the auto-provisioner that creates the `SheetRowSetting` row idempotently. Includes a "Common mistakes" section calling out the project's recurring pitfalls.

- **Phase 5a of Sheet Control v2 — per-row "Who" column override** (feat(p3)). Admins can now override Col B (the "Who" / responsible-actor label) per row in three languages, the same way they already override Col C (the field label). `SheetRowSetting` gains `who_tk / who_ru / who_en` CharFields with `Cyrillic_General_CI_AS` collation. Migration `0006_sheet_row_setting_who_override` is a pure AddField triple. SheetLabelColumn resolves `setting?.who?.[currentUserLang]` first and falls back to `t(rowConfig.default_who_key)`. SheetRowsTab gains a third "Who (TK / RU / EN)" inline-input column between Label and Tooltip; reuses the existing `debouncedSave`. `/sheet/` payload's `row_settings[fk]` gains a `who: {tk?, ru?, en?}` block (null when all three are blank). 3 new tests pass on MSSQL.

- **Phase 4b of Sheet Control v2 — SLA escalation cron for stuck shipments** (feat(p3), ADR-0005). Hourly mgmt command (`python manage.py notify_stuck_shipments`) posts per-shipment Notification rows to active admin / director / boss users when a stuck shipment crosses the 8 / 15 / 30 day thresholds. Re-runs are no-ops once notifications exist; crossing the next threshold creates a new notification under a new kind.
  - **Notification model** — three new `KIND_CHOICES`: `stuck_8d` (entered the orange tier), `stuck_15d` (entered red), `stuck_30d` (extended critical). Migration `0005_notification_stuck_kinds` is a single AlterField on `kind.choices`. Frontend NotificationKind union and any icon mapping should pick these up automatically since the field is choices-based; no additional schema work needed.
  - **`notify_stuck_shipments` mgmt command** — one query for the candidate stuck shipments (≥ lowest threshold = 8 d, `is_archived=False`, `phase != COMPLETE`), one query for the recipient set (active users with role in `admin / director / boss`), one query for existing `(user_id, kind, link)` tuples to dedupe against, then one `bulk_create(batch_size=500)` for the missing notifications. Idempotent. Args: `--dry-run` (lists up to 20 candidates without writing). The `link` is `/shipments/<id>` so the frontend's existing notification renderer routes the user straight to the detail page.
  - **Recipient policy** — mirrors `ShipmentViewSet._STUCK_VIEW_ROLES` (`admin / director / boss`); the dashboard and the cron stay in sync via the same role list. `warehouse_chief` and `export_manager` are NOT recipients (the dashboard view is also empty for them).
  - **Cron entry — hourly** (separate from the daily archive cron because threshold crossings should surface within ~1h, not next-day). Documented in the command docstring (Linux + Windows). Registration is a deploy concern.
  - **Tests (9 new cases on MSSQL, archive+stuck+notify total 30/30 pass)** — 8 d threshold creates one notification per recipient (admin + director, NOT warehouse_chief); 18 d shipment generates both `stuck_8d` AND `stuck_15d` for the same user; 35 d shipment generates all three kinds; second run produces no duplicates; archived shipments and terminal-phase shipments are excluded; under-threshold (5 d) shipments are skipped; `--dry-run` writes nothing.
  - **Out of scope (future)** — SMS/Telegram/WhatsApp delivery of these notifications (master plan §3.7 explicitly defers to in-app only for now). Frontend NotificationKind icon styling for `stuck_*` is a tiny follow-up if the existing default icon is unsuitable.

- **Phase 4a of Sheet Control v2 — Stuck Shipments dashboard** (feat(p3), ADR-0005). Read-only management view of operational shipments untouched for 4+ days. The page lives at `/director/stuck-shipments`, role-gated to admin / director / boss. Notification escalation (Phase 4b) will pull from the same backend filter and is a separate commit.
  - **Backend (export/views.py)** — `ShipmentViewSet` gains a `?stuck=true` query mode and a `_STUCK_VIEW_ROLES = ('admin', 'director', 'boss')` constant. Filter: `is_archived=False AND status.phase != 'COMPLETE' AND updated_at <= now - 4 days`, ordered `updated_at` ascending so the worst case surfaces at the top. Threshold lives in `_STUCK_THRESHOLD_DAYS = 4` (matches the lower bound of the dashboard's color scale: 4–7 yellow / 8–14 orange / 15+ red). Non-management roles silently get an empty page (uniform with the archive role gate).
  - **Frontend page (pages/director/StuckShipments.tsx)** — ProTable view with shipment_code (linked to detail), status, days_stuck (color-coded tag), country, customer, last_touched. `daysSince()` helper computed in the browser from `updated_at` so we don't need a backend annotation.
